 67:
 68: llvm::SPIRV::SelectionControl::SelectionControl
 69: getSelectionOperandForImm(int Imm) {
 70:   if (Imm == 2)
 71:     return SPIRV::SelectionControl::Flatten;
 72:   if (Imm == 1)
 73:     return SPIRV::SelectionControl::DontFlatten;
 74:   if (Imm == 0)
 75:     return SPIRV::SelectionControl::None;
 76:   llvm_unreachable("Invalid immediate");
 77: }

563: // Ensure that register classes correspond to pattern matching rules.
564: void SPIRVInstructionSelector::resetVRegsType(MachineFunction &MF) {
565:   if (HasVRegsReset == &MF)
566:     return;
567:   HasVRegsReset = &MF;
568:
569:   MachineRegisterInfo &MRI = MF.getRegInfo();
570:   for (unsigned I = 0, E = MRI.getNumVirtRegs(); I != E; ++I) {
571:     Register Reg = Register::index2VirtReg(I);
572:     LLT RegType = MRI.getType(Reg);
573:     if (RegType.isScalar())
574:       MRI.setType(Reg, LLT::scalar(64));
575:     else if (RegType.isPointer())
576:       MRI.setType(Reg, LLT::pointer(0, 64));
577:     else if (RegType.isVector())
578:       MRI.setType(Reg, LLT::fixed_vector(2, LLT::scalar(64)));
579:   }
580:   for (const auto &MBB : MF) {
581:     for (const auto &MI : MBB) {
582:       if (isPreISelGenericOpcode(MI.getOpcode()))
583:         GR.erase(&MI);
584:       if (MI.getOpcode() != SPIRV::ASSIGN_TYPE)
585:         continue;
586:
587:       Register DstReg = MI.getOperand(0).getReg();
588:       LLT DstType = MRI.getType(DstReg);
589:       Register SrcReg = MI.getOperand(1).getReg();
590:       LLT SrcType = MRI.getType(SrcReg);
591:       if (DstType != SrcType)
592:         MRI.setType(DstReg, MRI.getType(SrcReg));
593:
594:       const TargetRegisterClass *DstRC = MRI.getRegClassOrNull(DstReg);
595:       const TargetRegisterClass *SrcRC = MRI.getRegClassOrNull(SrcReg);
596:       if (DstRC != SrcRC && SrcRC)
597:         MRI.setRegClass(DstReg, SrcRC);
598:     }
599:   }
600: }

602: // Return true if the MachineInstr represents a constant register
603: static bool isConstReg(MachineRegisterInfo *MRI, MachineInstr *OpDef) {
604:
605:   SmallVector<MachineInstr *> Stack = {OpDef};
606:   SmallPtrSet<MachineInstr *, 4> Visited;
607:
608:   while (!Stack.empty()) {
609:     MachineInstr *MI = Stack.pop_back_val();
610:     MI = passCopy(MI, MRI);
611:     if (!Visited.insert(MI).second)
612:       continue;
613:     switch (MI->getOpcode()) {
614:     case TargetOpcode::G_INTRINSIC:
615:     case TargetOpcode::G_INTRINSIC_W_SIDE_EFFECTS:
616:     case TargetOpcode::G_INTRINSIC_CONVERGENT_W_SIDE_EFFECTS: {
617:       GIntrinsic *GIntr = cast<GIntrinsic>(MI);
618:       unsigned IntrID = GIntr->getIntrinsicID();
619:       if (IntrID != Intrinsic::spv_const_composite &&
620:           IntrID != Intrinsic::spv_undef)
621:         return false;
622:       continue;
623:     }
624:     case TargetOpcode::G_BUILD_VECTOR:
625:     case TargetOpcode::G_SPLAT_VECTOR:
626:       for (unsigned i = OpDef->getNumExplicitDefs();
627:            i < OpDef->getNumOperands(); i++) {
628:         if (!OpDef->getOperand(i).isReg())
629:           continue;
630:         MachineInstr *OpNestedDef =
631:             MRI->getVRegDef(OpDef->getOperand(i).getReg());
632:         Stack.push_back(OpNestedDef);
633:       }
634:       continue;
635:     case TargetOpcode::G_CONSTANT:
636:     case TargetOpcode::G_FCONSTANT:
637:     case TargetOpcode::G_IMPLICIT_DEF:
638:     case SPIRV::OpConstantTrue:
639:     case SPIRV::OpConstantFalse:
640:     case SPIRV::OpConstantI:
641:     case SPIRV::OpConstantF:
642:     case SPIRV::OpConstantComposite:
643:     case SPIRV::OpConstantCompositeContinuedINTEL:
644:     case SPIRV::OpConstantSampler:
645:     case SPIRV::OpConstantNull:
646:     case SPIRV::OpUndef:
647:     case SPIRV::OpConstantFunctionPointerINTEL:
648:       continue;
649:     default:
650:       return false;
651:     }
652:   }
653:   return true;
654: }
655:
656: // Return true if the virtual register represents a constant
657: static bool isConstReg(MachineRegisterInfo *MRI, Register OpReg) {
658:   if (MachineInstr *OpDef = MRI->getVRegDef(OpReg))
659:     return isConstReg(MRI, OpDef);
660:   return false;
661: }

771:
772: bool isDead(const MachineInstr &MI, const MachineRegisterInfo &MRI) {
773:   // If there are no definitions, then assume there is some other
774:   // side-effect that makes this instruction live.
775:   if (MI.getNumDefs() == 0)
776:     return false;
777:
778:   for (const auto &MO : MI.all_defs()) {
779:     Register Reg = MO.getReg();
780:     if (Reg.isPhysical()) {
781:       LLVM_DEBUG(dbgs() << "Not dead: def of physical register " << Reg);
782:       return false;
783:     }
784:     for (const auto &UseMI : MRI.use_nodbg_instructions(Reg)) {
785:       if (UseMI.getOpcode() != SPIRV::OpName) {
786:         LLVM_DEBUG(dbgs() << "Not dead: def " << MO << " has use in " << UseMI);
787:         return false;
788:       }
789:     }
790:   }
791:
792:   if (MI.getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::LOCAL_ESCAPE || MI.isFakeUse() ||
793:       MI.isLifetimeMarker()) {
794:     LLVM_DEBUG(
795:         dbgs()
796:         << "Not dead: Opcode is LOCAL_ESCAPE, fake use, or lifetime marker.\n");
797:     return false;
798:   }
799:   if (MI.isPHI()) {
800:     LLVM_DEBUG(dbgs() << "Dead: Phi instruction with no uses.\n");
801:     return true;
802:   }
803:
804:   // It is possible that the only side effect is that the instruction is
805:   // referenced in the global registry. If that is the only side effect, the
806:   // intrinsic is dead.
807:   if (MI.getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_INTRINSIC_W_SIDE_EFFECTS ||
808:       MI.getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_INTRINSIC_CONVERGENT_W_SIDE_EFFECTS) {
809:     const auto &Intr = cast<GIntrinsic>(MI);
810:     if (!intrinsicHasSideEffects(Intr.getIntrinsicID())) {
811:       LLVM_DEBUG(dbgs() << "Dead: Intrinsic with no real side effects.\n");
812:       return true;
813:     }
814:   }
815:
816:   if (MI.mayStore() || MI.isCall() ||
817:       (MI.mayLoad() && MI.hasOrderedMemoryRef()) || MI.isPosition() ||
818:       MI.isDebugInstr() || MI.isTerminator() || MI.isJumpTableDebugInfo()) {
819:     LLVM_DEBUG(dbgs() << "Not dead: instruction has side effects.\n");
820:     return false;
821:   }
822:
823:   if (isPreISelGenericOpcode(MI.getOpcode())) {
824:     // TODO: Is there a generic way to check if the opcode has side effects?
825:     LLVM_DEBUG(dbgs() << "Dead: Generic opcode with no uses.\n");
826:     return true;
827:   }
828:
829:   if (isOpcodeWithNoSideEffects(MI.getOpcode())) {
830:     LLVM_DEBUG(dbgs() << "Dead: known opcode with no side effects\n");
831:     return true;
832:   }
833:
834:   return false;
835: }

840:     Register Reg = MO.getReg();

863: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::select(MachineInstr &I) {
864:   resetVRegsType(*I.getParent()->getParent());
865:
866:   assert(I.getParent() && "Instruction should be in a basic block!");
867:   assert(I.getParent()->getParent() && "Instruction should be in a function!");
868:
869:   LLVM_DEBUG(dbgs() << "Checking if instruction is dead: " << I;);
870:   if (isDead(I, *MRI)) {
871:     LLVM_DEBUG(dbgs() << "Instruction is dead.\n");
872:     removeDeadInstruction(I);
873:     return true;
874:   }
875:
876:   Register Opcode = I.getOpcode();
877:   // If it's not a GMIR instruction, we've selected it already.
878:   if (!isPreISelGenericOpcode(Opcode)) {
879:     if (Opcode == SPIRV::ASSIGN_TYPE) { // These pseudos aren't needed any more.
880:       Register DstReg = I.getOperand(0).getReg();
881:       Register SrcReg = I.getOperand(1).getReg();
882:       auto *Def = MRI->getVRegDef(SrcReg);
883:       if (isTypeFoldingSupported(Def->getOpcode()) &&
884:           Def->getOpcode() != TargetOpcode::G_CONSTANT &&
885:           Def->getOpcode() != TargetOpcode::G_FCONSTANT) {
886:         if (Def->getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_SELECT) {
887:           Register SelectDstReg = Def->getOperand(0).getReg();
888:           bool SuccessToSelectSelect [[maybe_unused]] = selectSelect(
889:               SelectDstReg, GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(SelectDstReg), *Def);
890:           assert(SuccessToSelectSelect);
891:           GR.invalidateMachineInstr(Def);
892:           Def->eraseFromParent();
893:           MRI->replaceRegWith(DstReg, SelectDstReg);
894:           GR.invalidateMachineInstr(&I);
895:           I.eraseFromParent();
896:           return true;
897:         }
898:
899:         bool Res = selectImpl(I, *CoverageInfo);
900:         LLVM_DEBUG({
901:           if (!Res && Def->getOpcode() != TargetOpcode::G_CONSTANT) {
902:             dbgs() << "Unexpected pattern in ASSIGN_TYPE.\nInstruction: ";
903:             I.print(dbgs());
904:           }
905:         });
906:         assert(Res || Def->getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_CONSTANT);
907:         if (Res) {
908:           if (!isTriviallyDead(*Def, *MRI) && isDead(*Def, *MRI))
909:             DeadMIs.insert(Def);
910:           return Res;
911:         }
912:       }
913:       MRI->setRegClass(SrcReg, MRI->getRegClass(DstReg));
914:       MRI->replaceRegWith(SrcReg, DstReg);
915:       GR.invalidateMachineInstr(&I);
916:       I.eraseFromParent();
917:       return true;
918:     } else if (I.getNumDefs() == 1) {
919:       // Make all vregs 64 bits (for SPIR-V IDs).
920:       MRI->setType(I.getOperand(0).getReg(), LLT::scalar(64));
921:     }
922:     constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(I, TII, TRI, RBI);
923:     return true;
924:   }
925:
926:   if (DeadMIs.contains(&I)) {
927:     // if the instruction has been already made dead by folding it away
928:     // erase it
929:     LLVM_DEBUG(dbgs() << "Instruction is folded and dead.\n");
930:     removeDeadInstruction(I);
931:     DeadMIs.erase(&I);
932:     return true;
933:   }
934:
935:   if (I.getNumOperands() != I.getNumExplicitOperands()) {
936:     LLVM_DEBUG(errs() << "Generic instr has unexpected implicit operands\n");
937:     return false;
938:   }
939:
940:   // Common code for getting return reg+type, and removing selected instr
941:   // from parent occurs here. Instr-specific selection happens in spvSelect().
942:   bool HasDefs = I.getNumDefs() > 0;
943:   Register ResVReg = HasDefs ? I.getOperand(0).getReg() : Register(0);
944:   SPIRVTypeInst ResType = HasDefs ? GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(ResVReg) : nullptr;
945:   assert(!HasDefs || ResType || I.getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_GLOBAL_VALUE ||
946:          I.getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_IMPLICIT_DEF);
947:   if (spvSelect(ResVReg, ResType, I)) {
948:     if (HasDefs) // Make all vregs 64 bits (for SPIR-V IDs).
949:       for (unsigned i = 0; i < I.getNumDefs(); ++i)
950:         MRI->setType(I.getOperand(i).getReg(), LLT::scalar(64));
951:     GR.invalidateMachineInstr(&I);
952:     I.eraseFromParent();
953:     return true;
954:   }
955:   return false;
956: }

959:   switch (Opcode) {

1405:   BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(Opcode))
1406:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
1407:   return true;

1448: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectExtInst(Register ResVReg,
1449:                                              SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
1450:                                              MachineInstr &I,
1451:                                              const ExtInstList &Insts,
1452:                                              bool setMIFlags, bool useMISrc,
1453:                                              ArrayRef<Register> SrcRegs) const {
1454:
1455:   for (const auto &[InstructionSet, Opcode] : Insts) {
1456:     if (!STI.canUseExtInstSet(InstructionSet))
1457:       continue;
1458:     MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
1459:     auto MIB = BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpExtInst))
1460:                    .addDef(ResVReg)
1461:                    .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
1462:                    .addImm(static_cast<uint32_t>(InstructionSet))
1463:                    .addImm(Opcode);
1464:     if (setMIFlags)
1465:       MIB.setMIFlags(I.getFlags());
1466:     if (useMISrc) {
1467:       const unsigned NumOps = I.getNumOperands();
1468:       unsigned Index = 1;
1469:       if (Index < NumOps &&
1470:           I.getOperand(Index).getType() ==
1471:               MachineOperand::MachineOperandType::MO_IntrinsicID)
1472:         Index = 2;
1473:       for (; Index < NumOps; ++Index)
1474:         MIB.add(I.getOperand(Index));
1475:     } else {
1476:       for (Register SReg : SrcRegs) {
1477:         MIB.addUse(SReg);
1478:       }
1479:     }
1480:     MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
1481:     return true;
1482:   }
1483:   return false;
1484: }

1512:         .addDef(ResVReg)

1514:         .addImm(static_cast<uint32_t>(Ex.first))
1515:         .addImm(Opcode)
1516:         .add(I.getOperand(2))
1517:         .addUse(PointerVReg)
1518:         .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
1519:
1520:     BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpLoad))
1521:         .addDef(I.getOperand(1).getReg())
1522:         .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(PointeeTy))
1523:         .addUse(PointerVReg)
1524:         .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
1525:     return true;

1527:   return false;

1601: std::optional<SplitParts> SPIRVInstructionSelector::splitEvenOddLanes(
1602:     Register PopCountReg, unsigned ComponentCount, MachineInstr &I,
1603:     SPIRVTypeInst I32Type) const {
1604:   SplitParts Parts;
1605:
1606:   if (ComponentCount == 1) {
1607:     // ---- Scalar path: extract element 1 (high word) and element 0 (low word)
1608:     // ----
1609:     Parts.IsScalar = true;
1610:     Parts.Type = I32Type;
1611:     Parts.High = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(I32Type));
1612:     Parts.Low = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(I32Type));
1613:
1614:     bool ZeroAsNull = !STI.isShader();
1615:     Register IdxZero = GR.getOrCreateConstInt(0, I, I32Type, TII, ZeroAsNull);
1616:     Register IdxOne = GR.getOrCreateConstInt(1, I, I32Type, TII, ZeroAsNull);
1617:
1618:     if (!selectOpWithSrcs(Parts.High, I32Type, I, {PopCountReg, IdxOne},
1619:                           SPIRV::OpVectorExtractDynamic))
1620:       return std::nullopt;
1621:
1622:     if (!selectOpWithSrcs(Parts.Low, I32Type, I, {PopCountReg, IdxZero},
1623:                           SPIRV::OpVectorExtractDynamic))
1624:       return std::nullopt;
1625:
1626:   } else {
1627:     // ---- Vector path: shuffle odd lanes → High, even lanes → Low ----
1628:     MachineIRBuilder MIRBuilder(I);
1629:     Parts.IsScalar = false;
1630:     Parts.Type = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVVectorType(I32Type, ComponentCount,
1631:                                                MIRBuilder, /*IsSigned=*/false);
1632:     Parts.High = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(Parts.Type));
1633:     Parts.Low = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(Parts.Type));
1634:
1635:     // High = odd-indexed elements (1, 3, 5, …) — the upper 32-bit halves.
1636:     auto MIB = BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, I.getDebugLoc(),
1637:                        TII.get(SPIRV::OpVectorShuffle))
1638:                    .addDef(Parts.High)
1639:                    .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(Parts.Type))
1640:                    .addUse(PopCountReg)
1641:                    .addUse(PopCountReg);
1642:     for (unsigned J = 1; J < ComponentCount * 2; J += 2)
1643:       MIB.addImm(J);
1644:     MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
1645:
1646:     // Low = even-indexed elements (0, 2, 4, …) — the lower 32-bit halves.
1647:     MIB = BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, I.getDebugLoc(),
1648:                   TII.get(SPIRV::OpVectorShuffle))
1649:               .addDef(Parts.Low)
1650:               .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(Parts.Type))
1651:               .addUse(PopCountReg)
1652:               .addUse(PopCountReg);
1653:     for (unsigned J = 0; J < ComponentCount * 2; J += 2)
1654:       MIB.addImm(J);
1655:     MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
1656:   }
1657:
1658:   return Parts;
1659: }

1671:   SPIRVTypeInst I32VectorType =

1676:   Register ExtReg = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(ExtType));
1677:   // Always use OpUConvert to always use a 0 extend
1678:   if (!selectOpWithSrcs(ExtReg, ExtType, I, {OpReg}, SPIRV::OpUConvert))
1679:     return false;

1689:                                                 SPIRVTypeInst ResType,

1696: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectPopCount64(Register ResVReg,
1697:                                                 SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
1698:                                                 MachineInstr &I,
1699:                                                 Register SrcReg,
1700:                                                 unsigned Opcode) const {
1701:   unsigned ComponentCount = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentCount(ResType);
1702:   if (ComponentCount > 2)
1703:     return handle64BitOverflow(
1704:         ResVReg, ResType, I, SrcReg, Opcode,
1705:         [this](Register R, SPIRVTypeInst T, MachineInstr &I, Register S,
1706:                unsigned O) { return this->selectPopCount64(R, T, I, S, O); });
1707:
1708:   MachineIRBuilder MIRBuilder(I);
1709:
1710:   // ---- Types ----
1711:   SPIRVTypeInst I32Type = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVIntegerType(32, MIRBuilder);
1712:   SPIRVTypeInst VecI32Type = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVVectorType(
1713:       I32Type, 2 * ComponentCount, MIRBuilder, /*IsSigned=*/false);
1714:
1715:   // Converts 64 bit into and array of 32 bit, containing 2 elements.
1716:   Register Vec32 = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(VecI32Type));
1717:   if (!selectOpWithSrcs(Vec32, VecI32Type, I, {SrcReg}, SPIRV::OpBitcast))
1718:     return false;
1719:
1720:   // Apply popcount on each 32 bit lane
1721:   Register Pop32 = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(VecI32Type));
1722:   if (!selectPopCount32(Pop32, VecI32Type, I, Vec32, Opcode))
1723:     return false;
1724:
1725:   // Splits result into highbit lane and lowbit lane
1726:   auto MaybeParts = splitEvenOddLanes(Pop32, ComponentCount, I, I32Type);
1727:   if (!MaybeParts)
1728:     return false;
1729:   SplitParts &Parts = *MaybeParts;
1730:
1731:   // Sum high part and low part
1732:   unsigned OpAdd = Parts.IsScalar ? SPIRV::OpIAddS : SPIRV::OpIAddV;
1733:   Register Sum = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(Parts.Type));
1734:   if (!selectOpWithSrcs(Sum, Parts.Type, I, {Parts.High, Parts.Low}, OpAdd))
1735:     return false;
1736:
1737:   // Convert 32 bit sum into 64 bit scalar
1738:   bool IsSigned = GR.isScalarOrVectorSigned(ResType);
1739:   unsigned ConvOp = IsSigned ? SPIRV::OpSConvert : SPIRV::OpUConvert;
1740:   return selectOpWithSrcs(ResVReg, ResType, I, {Sum}, ConvOp);
1741: }

1743: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectPopCount(Register ResVReg,
1744:                                               SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
1745:                                               MachineInstr &I,
1746:                                               unsigned Opcode) const {
1747:   // Vulkan restricts OpBitCount to 32-bit integers or vectors of 32-bit
1748:   // integers unless VK_KHR_maintenance9 is enabled. Until VK_KHR_maintenance9
1749:   // is core we will not generate OpBitCount with any other types when
1750:   // targeting Vulkan.
1751:   if (!STI.getTargetTriple().isVulkanOS())
1752:     return selectUnOp(ResVReg, ResType, I, Opcode);
1753:
1754:   Register OpReg = I.getOperand(1).getReg();
1755:   SPIRVTypeInst OpType = GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(OpReg);
1756:   unsigned ExtOpcode = GR.isScalarOrVectorSigned(ResType) ? SPIRV::OpSConvert
1757:                                                           : SPIRV::OpUConvert;
1758:   switch (GR.getScalarOrVectorBitWidth(OpType)) {
1759:   case 8:
1760:   case 16:
1761:     return selectPopCount16(ResVReg, ResType, I, ExtOpcode, Opcode);
1762:   case 32:
1763:     return selectPopCount32(ResVReg, ResType, I, OpReg, Opcode);
1764:   case 64:
1765:     return selectPopCount64(ResVReg, ResType, I, OpReg, Opcode);
1766:   default:
1767:     return diagnoseUnsupported(I, "unsupported operand bit width for popcount");
1768:   }
1769: }
1770:
1771: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectUnOp(Register ResVReg,
1772:                                           SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
1773:                                           MachineInstr &I,
1774:                                           unsigned Opcode) const {
1775:   if (STI.isPhysicalSPIRV() && I.getOperand(1).isReg()) {
1776:     Register SrcReg = I.getOperand(1).getReg();
1777:     bool IsGV = false;
1778:     for (MachineRegisterInfo::def_instr_iterator DefIt =
1779:              MRI->def_instr_begin(SrcReg);
1780:          DefIt != MRI->def_instr_end(); DefIt = std::next(DefIt)) {
1781:       unsigned DefOpCode = DefIt->getOpcode();
1782:       if (DefOpCode == SPIRV::ASSIGN_TYPE || DefOpCode == TargetOpcode::COPY) {
1783:         // We need special handling to look through the type assignment or the
1784:         // COPY pseudo-op and see if this is a constant or a global.
1785:         if (auto *VRD = getVRegDef(*MRI, DefIt->getOperand(1).getReg()))
1786:           DefOpCode = VRD->getOpcode();
1787:       }
1788:       if (DefOpCode == TargetOpcode::G_GLOBAL_VALUE ||
1789:           DefOpCode == TargetOpcode::G_CONSTANT ||
1790:           DefOpCode == SPIRV::OpVariable || DefOpCode == SPIRV::OpConstantI) {
1791:         IsGV = true;
1792:         break;
1793:       }
1794:     }
1795:     if (IsGV) {
1796:       uint32_t SpecOpcode = 0;
1797:       switch (Opcode) {
1798:       case SPIRV::OpConvertPtrToU:
1799:         SpecOpcode = static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::Opcode::ConvertPtrToU);
1800:         break;
1801:       case SPIRV::OpConvertUToPtr:
1802:         SpecOpcode = static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::Opcode::ConvertUToPtr);
1803:         break;
1804:       }
1805:       if (SpecOpcode) {
1806:         BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, I.getDebugLoc(),
1807:                 TII.get(SPIRV::OpSpecConstantOp))
1808:             .addDef(ResVReg)
1809:             .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
1810:             .addImm(SpecOpcode)
1811:             .addUse(SrcReg)
1812:             .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
1813:         return true;
1814:       }
1815:     }
1816:   }
1817:   return selectOpWithSrcs(ResVReg, ResType, I, {I.getOperand(1).getReg()},
1818:                           Opcode);
1819: }
1820:
```
- EN: This range implements operational logic in helpers such as createVirtualRegister, selectOpWithSrcs, getScalarOrVectorComponentCount, selectPopCount64, translating backend policy into executable code.
- CN: 这一段实现了 createVirtualRegister、selectOpWithSrcs、getScalarOrVectorComponentCount、selectPopCount64 等操作逻辑，把后端策略落实为可执行的代码路径。

1833: static void addMemoryOperands(MachineMemOperand *MemOp,
1834:                               MachineInstrBuilder &MIB,
1835:                               MachineIRBuilder &MIRBuilder,
1836:                               SPIRVGlobalRegistry &GR) {
1837:   const SPIRVSubtarget *ST =
1838:       static_cast<const SPIRVSubtarget *>(&MIRBuilder.getMF().getSubtarget());
1839:   uint32_t SpvMemOp = static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::MemoryOperand::None);
1840:   if (MemOp->isVolatile())
1841:     SpvMemOp |= static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::MemoryOperand::Volatile);
1842:   if (MemOp->isNonTemporal())
1843:     SpvMemOp |= static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::MemoryOperand::Nontemporal);
1844:   // Aligned memory operand requires the Kernel capability.
1845:   if (!ST->isShader() && MemOp->getAlign().value())
1846:     SpvMemOp |= static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::MemoryOperand::Aligned);
1847:
1848:   [[maybe_unused]] MachineInstr *AliasList = nullptr;
1849:   [[maybe_unused]] MachineInstr *NoAliasList = nullptr;
1850:   if (ST->canUseExtension(SPIRV::Extension::SPV_INTEL_memory_access_aliasing)) {
1851:     if (auto *MD = MemOp->getAAInfo().Scope) {
1852:       AliasList = GR.getOrAddMemAliasingINTELInst(MIRBuilder, MD);
1853:       if (AliasList)
1854:         SpvMemOp |=
1855:             static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::MemoryOperand::AliasScopeINTELMask);
1856:     }
1857:     if (auto *MD = MemOp->getAAInfo().NoAlias) {
1858:       NoAliasList = GR.getOrAddMemAliasingINTELInst(MIRBuilder, MD);
1859:       if (NoAliasList)
1860:         SpvMemOp |=
1861:             static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::MemoryOperand::NoAliasINTELMask);
1862:     }
1863:   }
1864:
1865:   if (SpvMemOp != static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::MemoryOperand::None)) {
1866:     MIB.addImm(SpvMemOp);
1867:     if (SpvMemOp & static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::MemoryOperand::Aligned))
1868:       MIB.addImm(MemOp->getAlign().value());
1869:     if (AliasList)
1870:       MIB.addUse(AliasList->getOperand(0).getReg());
1871:     if (NoAliasList)
1872:       MIB.addUse(NoAliasList->getOperand(0).getReg());
1873:   }
1874: }

1876: static void addMemoryOperands(uint64_t Flags, MachineInstrBuilder &MIB) {
1877:   uint32_t SpvMemOp = static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::MemoryOperand::None);
1878:   if (Flags & MachineMemOperand::Flags::MOVolatile)
1879:     SpvMemOp |= static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::MemoryOperand::Volatile);
1880:   if (Flags & MachineMemOperand::Flags::MONonTemporal)
1881:     SpvMemOp |= static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::MemoryOperand::Nontemporal);
1882:
1883:   if (SpvMemOp != static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::MemoryOperand::None))
1884:     MIB.addImm(SpvMemOp);
1885: }

1887: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectLoad(Register ResVReg,
1888:                                           SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
1889:                                           MachineInstr &I) const {
1890:   unsigned OpOffset = isa<GIntrinsic>(I) ? 1 : 0;
1891:   Register Ptr = I.getOperand(1 + OpOffset).getReg();
1892:
1893:   auto *PtrDef = getVRegDef(*MRI, Ptr);
1894:   auto *IntPtrDef = dyn_cast<GIntrinsic>(PtrDef);
1895:   if (IntPtrDef &&
1896:       (IntPtrDef->getIntrinsicID() == Intrinsic::spv_resource_getbasepointer ||
1897:        IntPtrDef->getIntrinsicID() == Intrinsic::spv_resource_getpointer)) {
1898:
1899:     Register HandleReg = IntPtrDef->getOperand(2).getReg();
1900:     SPIRVTypeInst HandleType = GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(HandleReg);
1901:     if (HandleType->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpTypeImage) {
1902:       Register NewHandleReg =
1903:           MRI->createVirtualRegister(MRI->getRegClass(HandleReg));
1904:       auto *HandleDef = cast<GIntrinsic>(getVRegDef(*MRI, HandleReg));
1905:       if (!loadHandleBeforePosition(NewHandleReg, HandleType, *HandleDef, I)) {
1906:         return false;
1907:       }
1908:
1909:       Register IdxReg = IntPtrDef->getOperand(3).getReg();
1910:       return generateImageReadOrFetch(ResVReg, ResType, NewHandleReg, IdxReg,
1911:                                       I.getDebugLoc(), I);
1912:     }
1913:   }
1914:
1915:   MachineIRBuilder MIRBuilder(I);
1916:
1917:   if (I.getNumMemOperands()) {
1918:     const MachineMemOperand *MemOp = *I.memoperands_begin();
1919:     if (MemOp->isAtomic())
1920:       return selectAtomicLoad(ResVReg, ResType, I);
1921:   }
1922:
1923:   auto MIB = MIRBuilder.buildInstr(SPIRV::OpLoad)
1924:                  .addDef(ResVReg)
1925:                  .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
1926:                  .addUse(Ptr);
1927:   if (!I.getNumMemOperands()) {
1928:     assert(I.getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_INTRINSIC_W_SIDE_EFFECTS ||
1929:            I.getOpcode() ==
1930:                TargetOpcode::G_INTRINSIC_CONVERGENT_W_SIDE_EFFECTS);
1931:     addMemoryOperands(I.getOperand(2 + OpOffset).getImm(), MIB);
1932:   } else {
1933:     addMemoryOperands(*I.memoperands_begin(), MIB, MIRBuilder, GR);
1934:   }
1935:   MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
1936:   return true;
1937: }

2097:           .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))

2210: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectCopyMemory(MachineInstr &I,
2211:                                                 Register SrcReg) const {
2212:   MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
2213:   Register DstReg = I.getOperand(0).getReg();
2214:   SPIRVTypeInst DstTy = GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(DstReg);
2215:   SPIRVTypeInst SrcTy = GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(SrcReg);
2216:   if (GR.getPointeeType(DstTy) != GR.getPointeeType(SrcTy))
2217:     return diagnoseUnsupported(
2218:         I, "OpCopyMemory requires operands to have the same type");
2219:   uint64_t CopySize = getIConstVal(I.getOperand(2).getReg(), MRI);
2220:   SPIRVTypeInst PointeeTy = GR.getPointeeType(DstTy);
2221:   const Type *LLVMPointeeTy = GR.getTypeForSPIRVType(PointeeTy);
2222:   if (!LLVMPointeeTy)
2223:     return diagnoseUnsupported(
2224:         I, "Unable to determine pointee type size for OpCopyMemory");
2225:   const DataLayout &DL = I.getMF()->getFunction().getDataLayout();
2226:   if (CopySize != DL.getTypeStoreSize(const_cast<Type *>(LLVMPointeeTy)))
2227:     return diagnoseUnsupported(
2228:         I, "OpCopyMemory requires the size to match the pointee type size");
2229:   auto MIB = BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpCopyMemory))
2230:                  .addUse(DstReg)
2231:                  .addUse(SrcReg);
2232:   if (I.getNumMemOperands()) {
2233:     MachineIRBuilder MIRBuilder(I);
2234:     addMemoryOperands(*I.memoperands_begin(), MIB, MIRBuilder, GR);
2235:   }
2236:   MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
2237:   return true;
2238: }

2320: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectUnmergeValues(MachineInstr &I) const {
2321:   unsigned ArgI = I.getNumOperands() - 1;
2322:   Register SrcReg =
2323:       I.getOperand(ArgI).isReg() ? I.getOperand(ArgI).getReg() : Register(0);
2324:   SPIRVTypeInst SrcType =
2325:       SrcReg.isValid() ? GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(SrcReg) : nullptr;
2326:   if (!SrcType || SrcType->getOpcode() != SPIRV::OpTypeVector)
2327:     report_fatal_error(
2328:         "cannot select G_UNMERGE_VALUES with a non-vector argument");
2329:
2330:   SPIRVTypeInst ScalarType = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentType(SrcType);
2331:   MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
2332:   unsigned CurrentIndex = 0;
2333:   for (unsigned i = 0; i < I.getNumDefs(); ++i) {
2334:     Register ResVReg = I.getOperand(i).getReg();
2335:     SPIRVTypeInst ResType = GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(ResVReg);
2336:     if (!ResType) {
2337:       LLT ResLLT = MRI->getType(ResVReg);
2338:       assert(ResLLT.isValid());
2339:       if (ResLLT.isVector()) {
2340:         ResType = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVVectorType(
2341:             ScalarType, ResLLT.getNumElements(), I, TII);
2342:       } else {
2343:         ResType = ScalarType;
2344:       }
2345:       MRI->setRegClass(ResVReg, GR.getRegClass(ResType));
2346:       GR.assignSPIRVTypeToVReg(ResType, ResVReg, *GR.CurMF);
2347:     }
2348:
2349:     if (ResType->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpTypeVector) {
2350:       Register UndefReg = GR.getOrCreateUndef(I, SrcType, TII);
2351:       auto MIB =
2352:           BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpVectorShuffle))
2353:               .addDef(ResVReg)
2354:               .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
2355:               .addUse(SrcReg)
2356:               .addUse(UndefReg);
2357:       unsigned NumElements = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentCount(ResType);
2358:       for (unsigned j = 0; j < NumElements; ++j) {
2359:         MIB.addImm(CurrentIndex + j);
2360:       }
2361:       CurrentIndex += NumElements;
2362:       MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
2363:     } else {
2364:       auto MIB =
2365:           BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpCompositeExtract))
2366:               .addDef(ResVReg)
2367:               .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
2368:               .addUse(SrcReg)
2369:               .addImm(CurrentIndex);
2370:       CurrentIndex++;
2371:       MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
2372:     }
2373:   }
2374:   return true;
2375: }

2393: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectOverflowArith(Register ResVReg,
2394:                                                    SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
2395:                                                    MachineInstr &I,
2396:                                                    unsigned Opcode) const {
2397:   Type *ResTy = nullptr;
2398:   StringRef ResName;
2399:   if (!GR.findValueAttrs(&I, ResTy, ResName))
2400:     return diagnoseUnsupported(
2401:         I,
2402:         "Not enough info to select the arithmetic with overflow instruction");
2403:   if (!ResTy || !ResTy->isStructTy())
2404:     return diagnoseUnsupported(I,
2405:                                "Expect struct type result for the arithmetic "
2406:                                "with overflow instruction");
2407:   // "Result Type must be from OpTypeStruct. The struct must have two members,
2408:   // and the two members must be the same type."
2409:   Type *ResElemTy = cast<StructType>(ResTy)->getElementType(0);
2410:   ResTy = StructType::get(ResElemTy, ResElemTy);
2411:   // Build SPIR-V types and constant(s) if needed.
2412:   MachineIRBuilder MIRBuilder(I);
2413:   SPIRVTypeInst StructType = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVType(
2414:       ResTy, MIRBuilder, SPIRV::AccessQualifier::ReadWrite, false);
2415:   assert(I.getNumDefs() > 1 && "Not enought operands");
2416:   SPIRVTypeInst BoolType = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVBoolType(I, TII);
2417:   unsigned N = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentCount(ResType);
2418:   if (N > 1)
2419:     BoolType = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVVectorType(BoolType, N, I, TII);
2420:   Register BoolTypeReg = GR.getSPIRVTypeID(BoolType);
2421:   Register ZeroReg = buildZerosVal(ResType, I);
2422:   // A new virtual register to store the result struct.
2423:   Register StructVReg = MRI->createGenericVirtualRegister(LLT::scalar(64));
2424:   MRI->setRegClass(StructVReg, &SPIRV::IDRegClass);
2425:   // Build the result name if needed.
2426:   if (ResName.size() > 0)
2427:     buildOpName(StructVReg, ResName, MIRBuilder);
2428:   // Build the arithmetic with overflow instruction.
2429:   MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
2430:   auto MIB =
2431:       BuildMI(BB, MIRBuilder.getInsertPt(), I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(Opcode))
2432:           .addDef(StructVReg)
2433:           .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(StructType));
2434:   for (unsigned i = I.getNumDefs(); i < I.getNumOperands(); ++i)
2435:     MIB.addUse(I.getOperand(i).getReg());
2436:   MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
2437:   // Build instructions to extract fields of the instruction's result.
2438:   // A new virtual register to store the higher part of the result struct.
2439:   Register HigherVReg = MRI->createGenericVirtualRegister(LLT::scalar(64));
2440:   MRI->setRegClass(HigherVReg, &SPIRV::iIDRegClass);
2441:   for (unsigned i = 0; i < I.getNumDefs(); ++i) {
2442:     auto MIB =
2443:         BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpCompositeExtract))
2444:             .addDef(i == 1 ? HigherVReg : I.getOperand(i).getReg())
2445:             .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
2446:             .addUse(StructVReg)
2447:             .addImm(i);
2448:     MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
2449:   }
2450:   // Build boolean value from the higher part.
2451:   BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpINotEqual))
2452:       .addDef(I.getOperand(1).getReg())
2453:       .addUse(BoolTypeReg)
2454:       .addUse(HigherVReg)
2455:       .addUse(ZeroReg)
2456:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
2457:   return true;
2458: }

2461:                                                    SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
2462:                                                    MachineInstr &I) const {
2463:   assert(isa<GIntrinsic>(I) &&
2464:          "selectAtomicCmpXchg only handles the spv_cmpxchg intrinsic");
2465:   Register Ptr = I.getOperand(2).getReg();

2468:   Register MemSemNeqReg = I.getOperand(7).getReg();

2472:   Register ACmpRes = createVirtualRegister(SpvValTy, &GR, MRI, *I.getMF());
2473:   const DebugLoc &DL = I.getDebugLoc();
2474:   BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, DL, TII.get(SPIRV::OpAtomicCompareExchange))
2475:       .addDef(ACmpRes)
2476:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(SpvValTy))
2477:       .addUse(Ptr)
2478:       .addUse(ScopeReg)
2479:       .addUse(MemSemEqReg)
2480:       .addUse(MemSemNeqReg)
2481:       .addUse(Val)
2482:       .addUse(Cmp)
2483:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
2484:   SPIRVTypeInst BoolTy = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVBoolType(I, TII);
2485:   Register CmpSuccReg = createVirtualRegister(BoolTy, &GR, MRI, *I.getMF());
2486:   BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, DL, TII.get(SPIRV::OpIEqual))
2487:       .addDef(CmpSuccReg)
2488:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(BoolTy))
2489:       .addUse(ACmpRes)
2490:       .addUse(Cmp)
2491:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
2492:   Register TmpReg = createVirtualRegister(ResType, &GR, MRI, *I.getMF());
2493:   BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, DL, TII.get(SPIRV::OpCompositeInsert))
2494:       .addDef(TmpReg)
2495:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
2496:       .addUse(ACmpRes)
2497:       .addUse(GR.getOrCreateUndef(I, ResType, TII))
2498:       .addImm(0)
2499:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
2500:   BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, DL, TII.get(SPIRV::OpCompositeInsert))
2501:       .addDef(ResVReg)
2502:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
2503:       .addUse(CmpSuccReg)
2504:       .addUse(TmpReg)
2505:       .addImm(1)
2506:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
2507:   return true;

2521: static bool isASCastInGVar(MachineRegisterInfo *MRI, Register ResVReg) {
2522:   bool IsGRef = false;
2523:   bool IsAllowedRefs =
2524:       llvm::all_of(MRI->use_instructions(ResVReg), [&IsGRef](auto const &It) {
2525:         unsigned Opcode = It.getOpcode();

2572: // In SPIR-V address space casting can only happen to and from the Generic
2573: // storage class. We can also only cast Workgroup, CrossWorkgroup, or Function
2574: // pointers to and from Generic pointers. As such, we can convert e.g. from
2575: // Workgroup to Function by going via a Generic pointer as an intermediary. All
2576: // other combinations can only be done by a bitcast, and are probably not safe.
2577: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectAddrSpaceCast(Register ResVReg,
2578:                                                    SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
2579:                                                    MachineInstr &I) const {
2580:   MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
2581:   const DebugLoc &DL = I.getDebugLoc();
2582:
2583:   Register SrcPtr = I.getOperand(1).getReg();
2584:   SPIRVTypeInst SrcPtrTy = GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(SrcPtr);
2585:
2586:   // don't generate a cast for a null that may be represented by OpTypeInt
2587:   if (SrcPtrTy->getOpcode() != SPIRV::OpTypePointer ||
2588:       ResType->getOpcode() != SPIRV::OpTypePointer)
2589:     return BuildCOPY(ResVReg, SrcPtr, I);
2590:
2591:   SPIRV::StorageClass::StorageClass SrcSC = GR.getPointerStorageClass(SrcPtrTy);
2592:   SPIRV::StorageClass::StorageClass DstSC = GR.getPointerStorageClass(ResType);
2593:
2594:   if (isASCastInGVar(MRI, ResVReg)) {
2595:     // AddrSpaceCast uses within OpVariable and OpConstantComposite instructions
2596:     // are expressed by OpSpecConstantOp with an Opcode.
2597:     // TODO: maybe insert a check whether the Kernel capability was declared and
2598:     // so PtrCastToGeneric/GenericCastToPtr are available.
2599:     unsigned SpecOpcode =
2600:         DstSC == SPIRV::StorageClass::Generic && isGenericCastablePtr(SrcSC)
2601:             ? static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::Opcode::PtrCastToGeneric)
2602:             : (SrcSC == SPIRV::StorageClass::Generic &&
2603:                        isGenericCastablePtr(DstSC)
2604:                    ? static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::Opcode::GenericCastToPtr)
2605:                    : 0);
2606:     // TODO: OpConstantComposite expects i8*, so we are forced to forget a
2607:     // correct value of ResType and use general i8* instead. Maybe this should
2608:     // be addressed in the emit-intrinsic step to infer a correct
2609:     // OpConstantComposite type.
2610:     if (SpecOpcode) {
2611:       buildSpecConstantOp(I, ResVReg, SrcPtr, getUcharPtrTypeReg(I, DstSC),
2612:                           SpecOpcode)
2613:           .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
2614:     } else if (isGenericCastablePtr(SrcSC) && isGenericCastablePtr(DstSC)) {
2615:       MachineInstrBuilder MIB = buildConstGenericPtr(I, SrcPtr, SrcPtrTy);
2616:       MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
2617:       buildSpecConstantOp(
2618:           I, ResVReg, MIB->getOperand(0).getReg(), getUcharPtrTypeReg(I, DstSC),
2619:           static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::Opcode::GenericCastToPtr))
2620:           .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
2621:     }
2622:     return true;
2623:   }
2624:
2625:   // don't generate a cast between identical storage classes
2626:   if (SrcSC == DstSC)
2627:     return BuildCOPY(ResVReg, SrcPtr, I);
2628:
2629:   if ((SrcSC == SPIRV::StorageClass::Function &&
2630:        DstSC == SPIRV::StorageClass::Private) ||
2631:       (DstSC == SPIRV::StorageClass::Function &&
2632:        SrcSC == SPIRV::StorageClass::Private))
2633:     return BuildCOPY(ResVReg, SrcPtr, I);
2634:
2635:   // Casting from an eligible pointer to Generic.
2636:   if (DstSC == SPIRV::StorageClass::Generic && isGenericCastablePtr(SrcSC))
2637:     return selectUnOp(ResVReg, ResType, I, SPIRV::OpPtrCastToGeneric);
2638:   // Casting from Generic to an eligible pointer.
2639:   if (SrcSC == SPIRV::StorageClass::Generic && isGenericCastablePtr(DstSC))
2640:     return selectUnOp(ResVReg, ResType, I, SPIRV::OpGenericCastToPtr);
2641:   // Casting between 2 eligible pointers using Generic as an intermediary.

2649:         .addUse(SrcPtr)

2654:         .addUse(Tmp)
2655:         .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);

2676: static unsigned getFCmpOpcode(unsigned PredNum) {
2677:   auto Pred = static_cast<CmpInst::Predicate>(PredNum);
2678:   switch (Pred) {
2679:   case CmpInst::FCMP_OEQ:
2680:     return SPIRV::OpFOrdEqual;
2681:   case CmpInst::FCMP_OGE:
2682:     return SPIRV::OpFOrdGreaterThanEqual;
2683:   case CmpInst::FCMP_OGT:
2684:     return SPIRV::OpFOrdGreaterThan;
2685:   case CmpInst::FCMP_OLE:
2686:     return SPIRV::OpFOrdLessThanEqual;
2687:   case CmpInst::FCMP_OLT:
2688:     return SPIRV::OpFOrdLessThan;
2689:   case CmpInst::FCMP_ONE:
2690:     return SPIRV::OpFOrdNotEqual;
2691:   case CmpInst::FCMP_ORD:
2692:     return SPIRV::OpOrdered;
2693:   case CmpInst::FCMP_UEQ:
2694:     return SPIRV::OpFUnordEqual;
2695:   case CmpInst::FCMP_UGE:
2696:     return SPIRV::OpFUnordGreaterThanEqual;
2697:   case CmpInst::FCMP_UGT:
2698:     return SPIRV::OpFUnordGreaterThan;
2699:   case CmpInst::FCMP_ULE:
2700:     return SPIRV::OpFUnordLessThanEqual;
2701:   case CmpInst::FCMP_ULT:
2702:     return SPIRV::OpFUnordLessThan;
2703:   case CmpInst::FCMP_UNE:
2704:     return SPIRV::OpFUnordNotEqual;
2705:   case CmpInst::FCMP_UNO:
2706:     return SPIRV::OpUnordered;
2707:   default:
2708:     llvm_unreachable("Unknown predicate type for FCmp");
2709:   }
2710: }
2711:
2712: static unsigned getICmpOpcode(unsigned PredNum) {
2713:   auto Pred = static_cast<CmpInst::Predicate>(PredNum);
2714:   switch (Pred) {
2715:   case CmpInst::ICMP_EQ:
2716:     return SPIRV::OpIEqual;
2717:   case CmpInst::ICMP_NE:
2718:     return SPIRV::OpINotEqual;
2719:   case CmpInst::ICMP_SGE:
2720:     return SPIRV::OpSGreaterThanEqual;
2721:   case CmpInst::ICMP_SGT:
2722:     return SPIRV::OpSGreaterThan;
2723:   case CmpInst::ICMP_SLE:
2724:     return SPIRV::OpSLessThanEqual;
2725:   case CmpInst::ICMP_SLT:
2726:     return SPIRV::OpSLessThan;
2727:   case CmpInst::ICMP_UGE:
2728:     return SPIRV::OpUGreaterThanEqual;
2729:   case CmpInst::ICMP_UGT:
2730:     return SPIRV::OpUGreaterThan;
2731:   case CmpInst::ICMP_ULE:
2732:     return SPIRV::OpULessThanEqual;
2733:   case CmpInst::ICMP_ULT:
2734:     return SPIRV::OpULessThan;
2735:   default:
2736:     llvm_unreachable("Unknown predicate type for ICmp");
2737:   }
2738: }
2739:
2740: static unsigned getPtrCmpOpcode(unsigned Pred) {
2741:   switch (static_cast<CmpInst::Predicate>(Pred)) {
2742:   case CmpInst::ICMP_EQ:
2743:     return SPIRV::OpPtrEqual;
2744:   case CmpInst::ICMP_NE:
2745:     return SPIRV::OpPtrNotEqual;
2746:   default:
2747:     llvm_unreachable("Unknown predicate type for pointer comparison");
2748:   }
2749: }
2750:
2751: // Return the logical operation, or abort if none exists.
2752: static unsigned getBoolCmpOpcode(unsigned PredNum) {
2753:   auto Pred = static_cast<CmpInst::Predicate>(PredNum);
2754:   switch (Pred) {
2755:   case CmpInst::ICMP_EQ:
2756:     return SPIRV::OpLogicalEqual;
2757:   case CmpInst::ICMP_NE:
2758:     return SPIRV::OpLogicalNotEqual;
2759:   default:
2760:     llvm_unreachable("Unknown predicate type for Bool comparison");
2761:   }
2762: }
2763:
2764: static APFloat getZeroFP(const Type *LLVMFloatTy) {
2765:   if (!LLVMFloatTy)
2766:     return APFloat::getZero(APFloat::IEEEsingle());
2767:   switch (LLVMFloatTy->getScalarType()->getTypeID()) {
2768:   case Type::HalfTyID:
2769:     return APFloat::getZero(APFloat::IEEEhalf());
2770:   default:
2771:   case Type::FloatTyID:
2772:     return APFloat::getZero(APFloat::IEEEsingle());
2773:   case Type::DoubleTyID:
2774:     return APFloat::getZero(APFloat::IEEEdouble());
2775:   }
2776: }
2777:
2778: static APFloat getOneFP(const Type *LLVMFloatTy) {
2779:   if (!LLVMFloatTy)
2780:     return APFloat::getOne(APFloat::IEEEsingle());
2781:   switch (LLVMFloatTy->getScalarType()->getTypeID()) {
2782:   case Type::HalfTyID:
2783:     return APFloat::getOne(APFloat::IEEEhalf());
2784:   default:
2785:   case Type::FloatTyID:
2786:     return APFloat::getOne(APFloat::IEEEsingle());
2787:   case Type::DoubleTyID:
2788:     return APFloat::getOne(APFloat::IEEEdouble());
2789:   }
2790: }

2908: // Since pre-1.6 SPIRV has no integer dot implementation,
2909: // expand by piecewise multiplying and adding the results
2910: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectIntegerDotExpansion(
2911:     Register ResVReg, SPIRVTypeInst ResType, MachineInstr &I) const {
2912:   assert(I.getNumOperands() == 4);
2913:   assert(I.getOperand(2).isReg());
2914:   assert(I.getOperand(3).isReg());
2915:   MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
2916:
2917:   // Multiply the vectors, then sum the results
2918:   Register Vec0 = I.getOperand(2).getReg();
2919:   Register Vec1 = I.getOperand(3).getReg();
2920:   Register TmpVec = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(ResType));
2921:   SPIRVTypeInst VecType = GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(Vec0);
2922:
2923:   BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpIMulV))
2924:       .addDef(TmpVec)
2925:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(VecType))
2926:       .addUse(Vec0)
2927:       .addUse(Vec1)
2928:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
2929:
2930:   assert(VecType->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpTypeVector &&
2931:          GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentCount(VecType) > 1 &&
2932:          "dot product requires a vector of at least 2 components");
2933:
2934:   Register Res = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(ResType));
2935:   BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpCompositeExtract))
2936:       .addDef(Res)
2937:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
2938:       .addUse(TmpVec)
2939:       .addImm(0)
2940:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
```

3025: // Since pre-1.6 SPIRV has no DotProductInput4x8BitPacked implementation,
3026: // extract the elements of the packed inputs, multiply them and add the result
3027: // to the accumulator.
3028: template <bool Signed>
3029: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectDot4AddPackedExpansion(
3030:     Register ResVReg, SPIRVTypeInst ResType, MachineInstr &I) const {
3031:   assert(I.getNumOperands() == 5);
3032:   assert(I.getOperand(2).isReg());
3033:   assert(I.getOperand(3).isReg());
3034:   assert(I.getOperand(4).isReg());
3035:   MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
3036:
3037:   Register Acc = I.getOperand(2).getReg();
3038:   Register X = I.getOperand(3).getReg();
3039:   Register Y = I.getOperand(4).getReg();
3040:
3041:   SPIRVTypeInst EltType = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVIntegerType(8, I, TII);
3042:   auto ExtractOp =
3043:       Signed ? SPIRV::OpBitFieldSExtract : SPIRV::OpBitFieldUExtract;
3044:
3045:   bool ZeroAsNull = !STI.isShader();
3046:   // Extract the i8 element, multiply and add it to the accumulator
3047:   for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
3048:     // A[i]
3049:     Register AElt = MRI->createVirtualRegister(&SPIRV::IDRegClass);
3050:     BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(ExtractOp))
3051:         .addDef(AElt)
3052:         .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
3053:         .addUse(X)
3054:         .addUse(GR.getOrCreateConstInt(i * 8, I, EltType, TII, ZeroAsNull))
3055:         .addUse(GR.getOrCreateConstInt(8, I, EltType, TII, ZeroAsNull))
3056:         .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
3057:
3058:     // B[i]
3059:     Register BElt = MRI->createVirtualRegister(&SPIRV::IDRegClass);
3060:     BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(ExtractOp))
3061:         .addDef(BElt)
3062:         .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
3063:         .addUse(Y)
3064:         .addUse(GR.getOrCreateConstInt(i * 8, I, EltType, TII, ZeroAsNull))
3065:         .addUse(GR.getOrCreateConstInt(8, I, EltType, TII, ZeroAsNull))
3066:         .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
3067:
3068:     // A[i] * B[i]
3069:     Register Mul = MRI->createVirtualRegister(&SPIRV::IDRegClass);
3070:     BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpIMulS))
3071:         .addDef(Mul)
3072:         .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
3073:         .addUse(AElt)
3074:         .addUse(BElt)
3075:         .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
3076:
3077:     // Discard 24 highest-bits so that stored i32 register is i8 equivalent
3078:     Register MaskMul = MRI->createVirtualRegister(&SPIRV::IDRegClass);
3079:     BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(ExtractOp))
3080:         .addDef(MaskMul)
```

3103: /// Transform saturate(x) to clamp(x, 0.0f, 1.0f) as SPIRV
3104: /// does not have a saturate builtin.
3105: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectSaturate(Register ResVReg,
3106:                                               SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
3107:                                               MachineInstr &I) const {
3108:   assert(I.getNumOperands() == 3);
3109:   assert(I.getOperand(2).isReg());
3110:   MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
3111:   Register VZero = buildZerosValF(ResType, I);
3112:   Register VOne = buildOnesValF(ResType, I);
3113:
3114:   BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpExtInst))
3115:       .addDef(ResVReg)
3116:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
3117:       .addImm(static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::InstructionSet::GLSL_std_450))
3118:       .addImm(GL::FClamp)
3119:       .addUse(I.getOperand(2).getReg())
3120:       .addUse(VZero)
3121:       .addUse(VOne)
3122:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
3123:   return true;
3124: }

3126: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectSign(Register ResVReg,
3127:                                           SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
3128:                                           MachineInstr &I) const {
3129:   assert(I.getNumOperands() == 3);
3130:   assert(I.getOperand(2).isReg());
3131:   MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
3132:   Register InputRegister = I.getOperand(2).getReg();
3133:   SPIRVTypeInst InputType = GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(InputRegister);
3134:   auto &DL = I.getDebugLoc();
3135:
3136:   if (!InputType)
3137:     return diagnoseUnsupported(I, "Input Type could not be determined.");
3138:
3139:   bool IsFloatTy = GR.isScalarOrVectorOfType(InputRegister, SPIRV::OpTypeFloat);
3140:
3141:   unsigned SignBitWidth = GR.getScalarOrVectorBitWidth(InputType);
3142:   unsigned ResBitWidth = GR.getScalarOrVectorBitWidth(ResType);
3143:
3144:   bool NeedsConversion = IsFloatTy || SignBitWidth != ResBitWidth;
3145:
3146:   auto SignOpcode = IsFloatTy ? GL::FSign : GL::SSign;
3147:   Register SignReg = NeedsConversion
3148:                          ? MRI->createVirtualRegister(&SPIRV::IDRegClass)
3149:                          : ResVReg;
3150:
3151:   BuildMI(BB, I, DL, TII.get(SPIRV::OpExtInst))
3152:       .addDef(SignReg)
3153:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(InputType))
3154:       .addImm(static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::InstructionSet::GLSL_std_450))
3155:       .addImm(SignOpcode)
3156:       .addUse(InputRegister)
3157:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
3158:
3159:   if (NeedsConversion) {
3160:     auto ConvertOpcode = IsFloatTy ? SPIRV::OpConvertFToS : SPIRV::OpSConvert;
3161:     BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, DL, TII.get(ConvertOpcode))
3162:         .addDef(ResVReg)
3163:         .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
3164:         .addUse(SignReg)
3165:         .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
3166:   }
3167:
3168:   return true;
3169: }

3171: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectWaveOpInst(Register ResVReg,
3172:                                                 SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
3173:                                                 MachineInstr &I,
3174:                                                 unsigned Opcode) const {
3175:   MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
3176:   SPIRVTypeInst IntTy = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVIntegerType(32, I, TII);
3177:
3178:   auto BMI = BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(Opcode))
3179:                  .addDef(ResVReg)
3180:                  .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
3181:                  .addUse(GR.getOrCreateConstInt(SPIRV::Scope::Subgroup, I,
3182:                                                 IntTy, TII, !STI.isShader()));
3183:
3184:   for (unsigned J = 2; J < I.getNumOperands(); J++) {
3185:     BMI.addUse(I.getOperand(J).getReg());
3186:   }
3187:
3188:   BMI.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
3189:   return true;
3190: }

3192: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectBarrierInst(MachineInstr &I,
3193:                                                  unsigned Scope,
3194:                                                  unsigned MemSem,
3195:                                                  bool WithGroupSync) const {
3196:   auto BarrierType =

3209:          "Device Scope must set UniformMemory and ImageMemory semantic "

3252: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectWaveActiveAllEqual(Register ResVReg,
3253:                                                         SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
3254:                                                         MachineInstr &I) const {
3255:   MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
3256:   const DebugLoc &DL = I.getDebugLoc();
3257:
3258:   // Input to the intrinsic
3259:   Register InputReg = I.getOperand(2).getReg();
3260:   SPIRVTypeInst InputType = GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(InputReg);
3261:
3262:   // Determine if input is vector
3263:   unsigned NumElems = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentCount(InputType);
3264:   bool IsVector = NumElems > 1;
3265:
3266:   // Determine element types
3267:   SPIRVTypeInst ElemInputType = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentType(InputType);
3268:   SPIRVTypeInst ElemBoolType = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentType(ResType);
3269:
3270:   // Subgroup scope constant
3271:   SPIRVTypeInst IntTy = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVIntegerType(32, I, TII);
3272:   Register ScopeConst = GR.getOrCreateConstInt(SPIRV::Scope::Subgroup, I, IntTy,
3273:                                                TII, !STI.isShader());
3274:
3275:   // Scalar case
3276:   if (!IsVector) {
3277:     return selectWaveOpInst(ResVReg, ElemBoolType, I,
3278:                             SPIRV::OpGroupNonUniformAllEqual);
3279:   }
3280:
3281:   // Vector case
3282:   SmallVector<Register, 4> ElementResults;
3283:   ElementResults.reserve(NumElems);
3284:
3285:   for (unsigned Idx = 0; Idx < NumElems; ++Idx) {
3286:     // Extract element
3287:     Register ElemInput = InputReg;
3288:     Register Extracted =
3289:         MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(ElemInputType));
3290:
3291:     BuildMI(BB, I, DL, TII.get(SPIRV::OpCompositeExtract))
3292:         .addDef(Extracted)
3293:         .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ElemInputType))
3294:         .addUse(InputReg)
3295:         .addImm(Idx)
3296:         .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
3297:
3298:     ElemInput = Extracted;
3299:
3300:     // Emit per-element AllEqual
3301:     Register ElemResult =
3302:         MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(ElemBoolType));
3303:
3304:     BuildMI(BB, I, DL, TII.get(SPIRV::OpGroupNonUniformAllEqual))
3305:         .addDef(ElemResult)
3306:         .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ElemBoolType))
3307:         .addUse(ScopeConst)
3308:         .addUse(ElemInput)
3309:         .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
3310:
3311:     ElementResults.push_back(ElemResult);
3312:   }
3313:
3314:   // Reconstruct vector<bool>
3315:   auto MIB = BuildMI(BB, I, DL, TII.get(SPIRV::OpCompositeConstruct))
3316:                  .addDef(ResVReg)
3317:                  .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType));
3318:   for (Register R : ElementResults)
3319:     MIB.addUse(R);
3320:
3321:   MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
3322:
3323:   return true;
3324: }

3447:       .addDef(ResVReg)

3540: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectBitreverse16(Register ResVReg,
3541:                                                   SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
3542:                                                   MachineInstr &I,
3543:                                                   Register Op) const {
3544:   SPIRVTypeInst Int32Type = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVIntegerType(32, I, TII);
3545:   Register ShiftConst = GR.getOrCreateConstInt(16, I, Int32Type, TII);
3546:   unsigned ShiftOp = SPIRV::OpShiftRightLogicalS;
3547:
3548:   const unsigned N = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentCount(ResType);
3549:   const unsigned ExtendOpcode = GR.isScalarOrVectorSigned(ResType)
3550:                                     ? SPIRV::OpSConvert
3551:                                     : SPIRV::OpUConvert;
3552:
3553:   if (N > 1) {
3554:     Int32Type = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVVectorType(Int32Type, N, I, TII);
3555:     ShiftOp = SPIRV::OpShiftRightLogicalV;
3556:
3557:     // Vector shifts require a composite constant
3558:     const Register CompositeReg =
3559:         MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(Int32Type));
3560:     auto MIB = BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, I.getDebugLoc(),
3561:                        TII.get(SPIRV::OpConstantComposite))
3562:                    .addDef(CompositeReg)
3563:                    .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(Int32Type));
3564:     for (unsigned It = 0; It < N; ++It)
3565:       MIB.addUse(ShiftConst);
3566:     MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
3567:
3568:     ShiftConst = CompositeReg;
3569:   }
3570:
3571:   // Converts the i16 input to i32 (or vector of i32)
3572:   Register ExtReg = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(Int32Type));
3573:   if (!selectOpWithSrcs(ExtReg, Int32Type, I, {Op}, ExtendOpcode))
3574:     return false;
3575:
3576:   // Perform bitreverse on the i32 value
3577:   Register BitrevReg = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(Int32Type));
3578:   if (!selectBitreverseNative(BitrevReg, Int32Type, I, ExtReg))
3579:     return false;
3580:
3581:   // Shift the bit-reversed value to get the final result.
3582:   Register ShiftReg = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(Int32Type));
3583:   if (!selectOpWithSrcs(ShiftReg, Int32Type, I, {BitrevReg, ShiftConst},
3584:                         ShiftOp))
3585:     return false;
3586:
3587:   // Finally, convert the result back to i16 (or vector of i16).
3588:   return selectOpWithSrcs(ResVReg, ResType, I, {ShiftReg}, ExtendOpcode);
3589: }

3664:                                                   MachineInstr &I,
3665:                                                   Register SrcReg) const {
3666:   unsigned ComponentCount = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentCount(ResType);
3667:   if (ComponentCount > 2)
3668:     return handle64BitOverflow(
3669:         ResVReg, ResType, I, SrcReg, SPIRV::OpBitReverse,
3670:         [this](Register R, SPIRVTypeInst T, MachineInstr &I, Register S,
3671:                unsigned O) { return this->selectBitreverse64(R, T, I, S); });
3672:
3673:   MachineIRBuilder MIRBuilder(I);
3674:
3675:   SPIRVTypeInst I32Type = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVIntegerType(32, MIRBuilder);
3676:   SPIRVTypeInst VecI32Type = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVVectorType(
3677:       I32Type, 2 * ComponentCount, MIRBuilder, /*IsSigned=*/false);
3678:
3679:   // Converts 64 bit into and array of 32 bit, containing 2 elements.
3680:   Register Vec32 = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(VecI32Type));
3681:   if (!selectOpWithSrcs(Vec32, VecI32Type, I, {SrcReg}, SPIRV::OpBitcast))
3682:     return false;
3683:
3684:   // Apply bitreverse  on each 32 bit lane
3685:   Register Reverse32 = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(VecI32Type));
3686:   if (!selectBitreverseNative(Reverse32, VecI32Type, I, Vec32))
3687:     return false;
3688:
3689:   // Splits result into highbit lane and lowbit lane
3690:   auto MaybeParts = splitEvenOddLanes(Reverse32, ComponentCount, I, I32Type);
3691:   if (!MaybeParts)
3692:     return false;
3693:   SplitParts &Parts = *MaybeParts;
3694:
3695:   // Reversing a 64-bit value = reverse each 32-bit half AND swap them,
3696:   // so the old High word becomes lane 0 (low) and old Low becomes lane 1
3697:   // (high).
3698:   Register SwappedVec = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(VecI32Type));
3699:   if (!selectOpWithSrcs(SwappedVec, VecI32Type, I, {Parts.High, Parts.Low},
3700:                         SPIRV::OpCompositeConstruct))
3701:     return false;
3702:
3703:   // Groups 32 bit vector back to 64 bit scalar.
3704:   return selectOpWithSrcs(ResVReg, ResType, I, {SwappedVec}, SPIRV::OpBitcast);
3705: }

3707: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectBitreverseNative(Register ResVReg,
3708:                                                       SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
3709:                                                       MachineInstr &I,
3710:                                                       Register Op) const {
3711:   MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
3712:   BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpBitReverse))
3713:       .addDef(ResVReg)
3714:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
3715:       .addUse(Op)
3716:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
3717:   return true;

3719:
3720: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectBitreverse(Register ResVReg,
3721:                                                 SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
3722:                                                 MachineInstr &I) const {
3723:   Register OpReg = I.getOperand(1).getReg();
3724:
3725:   // TODO: Fix shader behavior in case of VK_KHR_maintenance9 extension is set
3726:   if (STI.isShader()) {
3727:     SPIRVTypeInst OpType = GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(OpReg);
3728:     switch (GR.getScalarOrVectorBitWidth(OpType)) {
3729:     case 8:
3730:     case 16:
3731:       return selectBitreverse16(ResVReg, ResType, I, OpReg);
3732:     case 32:
3733:       return selectBitreverseNative(ResVReg, ResType, I, OpReg);
3734:     case 64:
3735:       return selectBitreverse64(ResVReg, ResType, I, OpReg);
3736:     }
3737:     return SPIRVInstructionSelector::diagnoseUnsupported(
3738:         I, "G_BITREVERSE only support 16,32,64 bits.");
3739:   }
3740:
3741:   if (STI.canUseExtension(SPIRV::Extension::SPV_KHR_bit_instructions))
3742:     return selectBitreverseNative(ResVReg, ResType, I, OpReg);
3743:
3744:   // Expansion bitreverse using bit manipulation operations
3745:   // Algo: https://graphics.stanford.edu/~seander/bithacks.html#ReverseParallel
3746:   const unsigned BitWidth = GR.getScalarOrVectorBitWidth(ResType);
3747:   // TODO: add support for any bit width and bitwidth more than 64.
3748:   if (BitWidth > 64 || !isPowerOf2_32(BitWidth))
3749:     return false;
3750:
3751:   const unsigned N = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentCount(ResType);
3752:
3753:   unsigned AndOp = SPIRV::OpBitwiseAndS;
3754:   unsigned OrOp = SPIRV::OpBitwiseOrS;
3755:   unsigned ShlOp = SPIRV::OpShiftLeftLogicalS;
3756:   unsigned ShrOp = SPIRV::OpShiftRightLogicalS;
3757:   if (N > 1) {
3758:     AndOp = SPIRV::OpBitwiseAndV;
3759:     OrOp = SPIRV::OpBitwiseOrV;
3760:     ShlOp = SPIRV::OpShiftLeftLogicalV;
3761:     ShrOp = SPIRV::OpShiftRightLogicalV;
3762:   }
3763:
3764:   // Helper, one swap per step: ((input>>shift)&mask)|((input&mask)<<shift),
3765:   // RPN: input shift >> mask & input mask & shift << |
3766:   auto SwapBits = [&](const Register Input, const uint64_t Mask,
3767:                       const unsigned Shift) -> Register {
3768:     auto CreateConst = [&](const uint64_t Value) -> Register {
3769:       if (N == 1)
3770:         return GR.getOrCreateConstInt(
3771:             Value, I, GR.retrieveScalarOrVectorIntType(ResType), TII);
3772:       return GR.getOrCreateConstVector(Value, I, ResType, TII);
3773:     };
3774:
3775:     Register MaskReg = CreateConst(Mask);
3776:     Register ShiftReg = CreateConst(Shift);
3777:     Register T1 = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(ResType));
3778:     Register T2 = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(ResType));
3779:     Register T3 = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(ResType));
3780:     Register T4 = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(ResType));
```
- EN: This range implements operational logic in helpers such as createVirtualRegister, push_back, getScalarOrVectorComponentCount, selectBitreverse64, translating backend policy into executable code.

3785:         !selectOpWithSrcs(T3, ResType, I, {Input, MaskReg}, AndOp) ||

3807:                                             SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
3808:                                             MachineInstr &I) const {
3809:   // There is no way to implement `freeze` correctly without support on SPIR-V
3810:   // standard side, but we may at least address a simple (static) case when
3811:   // undef/poison value presence is obvious. The main benefit of even
3812:   // incomplete `freeze` support is preventing of translation from crashing due
3813:   // to lack of support on legalization and instruction selection steps.
3814:   if (!I.getOperand(0).isReg() || !I.getOperand(1).isReg())
3815:     return false;
3816:   Register OpReg = I.getOperand(1).getReg();
3817:   if (MachineInstr *Def = MRI->getVRegDef(OpReg)) {
3818:     if (Def->getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::COPY)
3819:       Def = MRI->getVRegDef(Def->getOperand(1).getReg());
3820:     Register Reg;
3821:     switch (Def->getOpcode()) {
3822:     case SPIRV::ASSIGN_TYPE:
3823:       if (MachineInstr *AssignToDef =
3824:               MRI->getVRegDef(Def->getOperand(1).getReg())) {
3825:         if (AssignToDef->getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_IMPLICIT_DEF)
3826:           Reg = Def->getOperand(2).getReg();
3827:       }
3828:       break;
3829:     case SPIRV::OpUndef:
3830:       Reg = Def->getOperand(1).getReg();
3831:       break;
3832:     }
3833:     unsigned DestOpCode;
3834:     if (Reg.isValid()) {
3835:       DestOpCode = SPIRV::OpConstantNull;
3836:     } else {
3837:       DestOpCode = TargetOpcode::COPY;
3838:       Reg = OpReg;
3839:     }
3840:     BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(DestOpCode))
3841:         .addDef(I.getOperand(0).getReg())
3842:         .addUse(Reg)
3843:         .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
3844:     return true;
3845:   }
3846:   return false;

3849: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectBuildVector(Register ResVReg,
3850:                                                  SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
3851:                                                  MachineInstr &I) const {
3852:   unsigned N = 0;
3853:   if (ResType->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpTypeVector)
3854:     N = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentCount(ResType);
3855:   else if (ResType->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpTypeArray)
3856:     N = getArrayComponentCount(MRI, ResType);
3857:   else
3858:     report_fatal_error("Cannot select G_BUILD_VECTOR with a non-vector result");
3859:   if (I.getNumExplicitOperands() - I.getNumExplicitDefs() != N)
3860:     report_fatal_error("G_BUILD_VECTOR and the result type are inconsistent");
3861:
3862:   // check if we may construct a constant vector
3863:   bool IsConst = true;
3864:   for (unsigned i = I.getNumExplicitDefs();
3865:        i < I.getNumExplicitOperands() && IsConst; ++i)
3866:     if (!isConstReg(MRI, I.getOperand(i).getReg()))
3867:       IsConst = false;
3868:
3869:   if (!IsConst && N < 2)
3870:     return diagnoseUnsupported(
3871:         I, "There must be at least two constituent operands in a vector");
3872:
3873:   MRI->setRegClass(ResVReg, GR.getRegClass(ResType));
3874:   auto MIB = BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, I.getDebugLoc(),
3875:                      TII.get(IsConst ? SPIRV::OpConstantComposite
3876:                                      : SPIRV::OpCompositeConstruct))
3877:                  .addDef(ResVReg)
3878:                  .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType));
3879:   for (unsigned i = I.getNumExplicitDefs(); i < I.getNumExplicitOperands(); ++i)
3880:     MIB.addUse(I.getOperand(i).getReg());
3881:   MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
3882:   return true;
3883: }

4118:   SPIRVTypeInst Type = GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(Reg);
4119:   if (!Type)
4120:     return false;
4121:   SPIRVTypeInst CompType = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentType(Type);
4122:   if (!CompType || CompType->getOpcode() != SPIRV::OpTypeInt)
4123:     return false;
4124:
4125:   auto IsZero = [this](Register Reg) {
4126:     MachineInstr *Def = getDefInstrMaybeConstant(Reg, MRI);
4127:     if (!Def)
4128:       return false;
4129:
4130:     if (Def->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpConstantNull)
4131:       return true;
4132:
4133:     if (Def->getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_CONSTANT ||
4134:         Def->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpConstantI)
4135:       return getIConstVal(Reg, MRI) == 0;
4136:
4137:     return false;
4138:   };
4139:
4140:   if (IsZero(Reg))
4141:     return true;
4142:
4143:   MachineInstr *Def = MRI->getVRegDef(Reg);
4144:   if (!Def)
4145:     return false;
4146:
4147:   if (Def->getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_BUILD_VECTOR ||
4148:       (Def->getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_INTRINSIC_W_SIDE_EFFECTS &&
4149:        cast<GIntrinsic>(Def)->getIntrinsicID() ==
4150:            Intrinsic::spv_const_composite)) {
4151:     unsigned StartOp = Def->getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_BUILD_VECTOR ? 1 : 2;
4152:     for (unsigned i = StartOp; i < Def->getNumOperands(); ++i) {
4153:       if (!IsZero(Def->getOperand(i).getReg()))
4154:         return false;
4155:     }
4156:     return true;
4157:   }
4158:
4159:   return false;
4160: }

4162: Register SPIRVInstructionSelector::buildZerosValF(SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
4163:                                                   MachineInstr &I) const {
4164:   // OpenCL uses nulls for Zero. In HLSL we don't use null constants.
4165:   bool ZeroAsNull = !STI.isShader();
4166:   APFloat VZero = getZeroFP(GR.getTypeForSPIRVType(ResType));
4167:   if (ResType->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpTypeVector)
4168:     return GR.getOrCreateConstVector(VZero, I, ResType, TII, ZeroAsNull);
4169:   return GR.getOrCreateConstFP(VZero, I, ResType, TII, ZeroAsNull);
4170: }

4172: Register SPIRVInstructionSelector::buildOnesValF(SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
4173:                                                  MachineInstr &I) const {
4174:   // OpenCL uses nulls for Zero. In HLSL we don't use null constants.
4175:   bool ZeroAsNull = !STI.isShader();
4176:   APFloat VOne = getOneFP(GR.getTypeForSPIRVType(ResType));
4177:   if (ResType->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpTypeVector)
4178:     return GR.getOrCreateConstVector(VOne, I, ResType, TII, ZeroAsNull);
4179:   return GR.getOrCreateConstFP(VOne, I, ResType, TII, ZeroAsNull);

4182: Register SPIRVInstructionSelector::buildOnesVal(bool AllOnes,
4183:                                                 SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
4184:                                                 MachineInstr &I) const {
4185:   unsigned BitWidth = GR.getScalarOrVectorBitWidth(ResType);
4186:   APInt One =
4187:       AllOnes ? APInt::getAllOnes(BitWidth) : APInt::getOneBitSet(BitWidth, 0);
4188:   if (ResType->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpTypeVector)
4189:     return GR.getOrCreateConstVector(One, I, ResType, TII);
4190:   return GR.getOrCreateConstInt(One, I, ResType, TII);
4191: }

4242: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectBoolToInt(Register ResVReg,
4243:                                                SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
4244:                                                Register BooleanVReg,
4245:                                                MachineInstr &InsertAt,
4246:                                                bool IsSigned) const {

4257:       .addUse(BooleanVReg)
4258:       .addUse(OneReg)
4259:       .addUse(ZeroReg)
4260:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);

4300: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectSUCmp(Register ResVReg,
4301:                                            SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
4302:                                            MachineInstr &I,
4303:                                            bool IsSigned) const {
4304:   MachineIRBuilder MIRBuilder(I);
4305:   MachineRegisterInfo *MRI = MIRBuilder.getMRI();
4306:   MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
4307:   // Ensure we have bool.
4308:   SPIRVTypeInst BoolType = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVBoolType(I, TII);
4309:   unsigned N = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentCount(ResType);
4310:   if (N > 1)
4311:     BoolType = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVVectorType(BoolType, N, I, TII);
4312:   Register BoolTypeReg = GR.getSPIRVTypeID(BoolType);
4313:   // Build less-than-equal and less-than.
4314:   Register IsLessEqReg =
4315:       createVirtualRegister(BoolType, &GR, MRI, MIRBuilder.getMF());
4316:   BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(),
4317:           TII.get(IsSigned ? SPIRV::OpSLessThanEqual : SPIRV::OpULessThanEqual))
4318:       .addDef(IsLessEqReg)
4319:       .addUse(BoolTypeReg)
4320:       .addUse(I.getOperand(1).getReg())
4321:       .addUse(I.getOperand(2).getReg())
4322:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
4323:   Register IsLessReg =
4324:       createVirtualRegister(BoolType, &GR, MRI, MIRBuilder.getMF());
4325:   BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(),
4326:           TII.get(IsSigned ? SPIRV::OpSLessThan : SPIRV::OpULessThan))
4327:       .addDef(IsLessReg)
4328:       .addUse(BoolTypeReg)
4329:       .addUse(I.getOperand(1).getReg())
4330:       .addUse(I.getOperand(2).getReg())
4331:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
4332:   // Build selects.
4333:   Register ResTypeReg = GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType);
4334:   Register NegOneOrZeroReg =
4335:       MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(ResType));
4336:   MRI->setType(NegOneOrZeroReg, LLT::scalar(64));
4337:   GR.assignSPIRVTypeToVReg(ResType, NegOneOrZeroReg, MIRBuilder.getMF());
4338:   unsigned SelectOpcode =
4339:       N > 1 ? SPIRV::OpSelectVIVCond : SPIRV::OpSelectSISCond;
4340:   BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SelectOpcode))

4357: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectIntToBool(Register IntReg,
4358:                                                Register ResVReg,
4359:                                                MachineInstr &I,
4360:                                                SPIRVTypeInst IntTy,
4361:                                                SPIRVTypeInst BoolTy) const {
4362:   // To truncate to a bool, we use OpBitwiseAnd 1 and OpINotEqual to zero.
4363:   Register BitIntReg = createVirtualRegister(IntTy, &GR, MRI, MRI->getMF());
4364:   bool IsVectorTy = IntTy->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpTypeVector;
4365:   unsigned Opcode = IsVectorTy ? SPIRV::OpBitwiseAndV : SPIRV::OpBitwiseAndS;
4366:   Register Zero = buildZerosVal(IntTy, I);
4367:   Register One = buildOnesVal(false, IntTy, I);
4368:   MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
4369:   BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(Opcode))
4370:       .addDef(BitIntReg)
4371:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(IntTy))
4372:       .addUse(IntReg)
4373:       .addUse(One)
4374:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
4375:   BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpINotEqual))
4376:       .addDef(ResVReg)
4377:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(BoolTy))
4378:       .addUse(BitIntReg)
4379:       .addUse(Zero)
4380:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
4381:   return true;

4384: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectTrunc(Register ResVReg,
4385:                                            SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
4386:                                            MachineInstr &I) const {
4387:   Register IntReg = I.getOperand(1).getReg();
4388:   const SPIRVTypeInst ArgType = GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(IntReg);

4398: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectConst(Register ResVReg,
4399:                                            SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
4400:                                            MachineInstr &I) const {
4401:   unsigned Opcode = I.getOpcode();
4402:   unsigned TpOpcode = ResType->getOpcode();
4403:   Register Reg;
4404:   if (TpOpcode == SPIRV::OpTypePointer || TpOpcode == SPIRV::OpTypeEvent) {
4405:     assert(Opcode == TargetOpcode::G_CONSTANT &&
4406:            I.getOperand(1).getCImm()->isZero());
4407:     MachineBasicBlock &DepMBB = I.getMF()->front();
4408:     MachineIRBuilder MIRBuilder(DepMBB, DepMBB.getFirstNonPHI());
4409:     Reg = GR.getOrCreateConstNullPtr(MIRBuilder, ResType);
4410:   } else if (Opcode == TargetOpcode::G_FCONSTANT) {
4411:     Reg = GR.getOrCreateConstFP(I.getOperand(1).getFPImm()->getValue(), I,
4412:                                 ResType, TII, !STI.isShader());
4413:   } else {
4414:     Reg = GR.getOrCreateConstInt(I.getOperand(1).getCImm()->getValue(), I,
4415:                                  ResType, TII, !STI.isShader());
4416:   }
4417:   return Reg == ResVReg ? true : BuildCOPY(ResVReg, Reg, I);
4418: }

4430: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectInsertVal(Register ResVReg,
4431:                                                SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
4432:                                                MachineInstr &I) const {
4433:   MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
4434:   auto MIB = BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpCompositeInsert))
4435:                  .addDef(ResVReg)
4436:                  .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
4437:                  // object to insert
4438:                  .addUse(I.getOperand(3).getReg())
4439:                  // composite to insert into
4440:                  .addUse(I.getOperand(2).getReg());
4441:   for (unsigned i = 4; i < I.getNumOperands(); i++)
4442:     MIB.addImm(foldImm(I.getOperand(i), MRI));
4443:   MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
4444:   return true;
4445: }
