```cpp
4502: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectGEP(Register ResVReg,
4503:                                          SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
4504:                                          MachineInstr &I) const {
4505:   const bool IsGEPInBounds = I.getOperand(2).getImm();
4506:
4507:   // OpAccessChain could be used for OpenCL, but the SPIRV-LLVM Translator only
4508:   // relies on PtrAccessChain, so we'll try not to deviate. For Vulkan however,
4509:   // we have to use Op[InBounds]AccessChain.
4510:   const unsigned Opcode = STI.isLogicalSPIRV()
4511:                               ? (IsGEPInBounds ? SPIRV::OpInBoundsAccessChain
4512:                                                : SPIRV::OpAccessChain)
4513:                               : (IsGEPInBounds ? SPIRV::OpInBoundsPtrAccessChain
4514:                                                : SPIRV::OpPtrAccessChain);
4515:
4516:   auto Res = BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(Opcode))
4517:                  .addDef(ResVReg)
4518:                  .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
4519:                  // Object to get a pointer to.
4520:                  .addUse(I.getOperand(3).getReg());
4521:   assert(
4522:       (Opcode == SPIRV::OpPtrAccessChain ||
4523:        Opcode == SPIRV::OpInBoundsPtrAccessChain ||
4524:        (getImm(I.getOperand(4), MRI) && foldImm(I.getOperand(4), MRI) == 0)) &&
4525:       "Cannot translate GEP to OpAccessChain. First index must be 0.");
4526:
4527:   // Adding indices.
4528:   const unsigned StartingIndex =
4529:       (Opcode == SPIRV::OpAccessChain || Opcode == SPIRV::OpInBoundsAccessChain)
4530:           ? 5
4531:           : 4;
4532:   for (unsigned i = StartingIndex; i < I.getNumExplicitOperands(); ++i)
4533:     Res.addUse(I.getOperand(i).getReg());
4534:   Res.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
4535:   return true;
4536: }

4538: // Maybe wrap a value into OpSpecConstantOp
4539: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::wrapIntoSpecConstantOp(
4540:     MachineInstr &I, SmallVector<Register> &CompositeArgs) const {
4541:   unsigned Lim = I.getNumExplicitOperands();
4542:   for (unsigned i = I.getNumExplicitDefs() + 1; i < Lim; ++i) {
4543:     Register OpReg = I.getOperand(i).getReg();
4544:     MachineInstr *OpDefine = MRI->getVRegDef(OpReg);
4545:     SPIRVTypeInst OpType = GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(OpReg);
4546:     if (!OpDefine || !OpType || isConstReg(MRI, OpDefine) ||
4547:         OpDefine->getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_ADDRSPACE_CAST ||
4548:         OpDefine->getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_INTTOPTR ||
4549:         GR.isAggregateType(OpType)) {
4550:       // The case of G_ADDRSPACE_CAST inside spv_const_composite() is processed
4551:       // by selectAddrSpaceCast(), and G_INTTOPTR is processed by selectUnOp()
4552:       CompositeArgs.push_back(OpReg);
4553:       continue;
4554:     }
4555:     MachineFunction *MF = I.getMF();
4556:     Register WrapReg = GR.find(OpDefine, MF);
4557:     if (WrapReg.isValid()) {
4558:       CompositeArgs.push_back(WrapReg);
4559:       continue;
4560:     }
4561:     // Create a new register for the wrapper
4562:     WrapReg = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(OpType));
4563:     CompositeArgs.push_back(WrapReg);
4564:     // Decorate the wrapper register and generate a new instruction
4565:     MRI->setType(WrapReg, LLT::pointer(0, 64));
4566:     GR.assignSPIRVTypeToVReg(OpType, WrapReg, *MF);
4567:     auto MIB = BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, I.getDebugLoc(),
4568:                        TII.get(SPIRV::OpSpecConstantOp))
4569:                    .addDef(WrapReg)
4570:                    .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(OpType))
4571:                    .addImm(static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::Opcode::Bitcast))
4572:                    .addUse(OpReg);
4573:     GR.add(OpDefine, MIB);
4574:     MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
4575:   }
4576:   return true;
4577: }
4578:
4579: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectDerivativeInst(
4580:     Register ResVReg, SPIRVTypeInst ResType, MachineInstr &I,
4581:     const unsigned DPdOpCode) const {
4582:   // TODO: This should check specifically for Fragment Execution Model, but STI
4583:   // doesn't provide that information yet. See #167562
4584:   if (!errorIfInstrOutsideShader(I))
4585:     return false;
4586:
4587:   // If the arg/result types are half then we need to wrap the instr in
4588:   // conversions to float
4589:   // This case occurs because a half arg/result is legal in HLSL but not spirv.
4590:   Register SrcReg = I.getOperand(2).getReg();
4591:   SPIRVTypeInst SrcType = GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(SrcReg);
4592:   unsigned BitWidth = std::min(GR.getScalarOrVectorBitWidth(SrcType),
4593:                                GR.getScalarOrVectorBitWidth(ResType));
4594:   if (BitWidth == 32)
4595:     return BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(DPdOpCode))
4596:         .addDef(ResVReg)
4597:         .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
4598:         .addUse(I.getOperand(2).getReg());
4599:
4600:   MachineIRBuilder MIRBuilder(I);
4601:   unsigned componentCount = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentCount(SrcType);
4602:   SPIRVTypeInst F32ConvertTy = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVFloatType(32, I, TII);
4603:   if (componentCount != 1)
4604:     F32ConvertTy = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVVectorType(F32ConvertTy, componentCount,
4605:                                                  MIRBuilder, false);
4606:
4607:   const TargetRegisterClass *RegClass = GR.getRegClass(SrcType);
4608:   Register ConvertToVReg = MRI->createVirtualRegister(RegClass);
4609:   Register DpdOpVReg = MRI->createVirtualRegister(RegClass);
4610:
4611:   BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpFConvert))
4612:       .addDef(ConvertToVReg)
4613:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(F32ConvertTy))
4614:       .addUse(SrcReg)
4615:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
4616:   BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(DPdOpCode))
4617:       .addDef(DpdOpVReg)
4618:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(F32ConvertTy))
4619:       .addUse(ConvertToVReg)
4620:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
```
- EN: This range implements operational logic in helpers such as addUse, constrainAllUses, selectExtractVal, getParent, translating backend policy into executable code.
- CN: 这一段实现了 addUse、constrainAllUses、selectExtractVal、getParent 等操作逻辑，把后端策略落实为可执行的代码路径。

```cpp
4624:       .addUse(DpdOpVReg)

4629: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectIntrinsic(Register ResVReg,
4630:                                                SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
4631:                                                MachineInstr &I) const {
4632:   MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();

4634:   switch (IID) {
4635:   case Intrinsic::spv_load:
4636:     return selectLoad(ResVReg, ResType, I);
4637:   case Intrinsic::spv_atomic_load:
4638:     return selectAtomicLoad(ResVReg, ResType, I);
4639:   case Intrinsic::spv_store:
4640:     return selectStore(I);
4641:   case Intrinsic::spv_atomic_store:
4642:     return selectAtomicStore(I);
4643:   case Intrinsic::spv_extractv:
4644:     return selectExtractVal(ResVReg, ResType, I);
4645:   case Intrinsic::spv_insertv:

4647:   case Intrinsic::spv_extractelt:
4648:     return selectExtractElt(ResVReg, ResType, I);
4649:   case Intrinsic::spv_insertelt:
4650:     return selectInsertElt(ResVReg, ResType, I);
4651:   case Intrinsic::spv_gep:
4652:     return selectGEP(ResVReg, ResType, I);
4653:   case Intrinsic::spv_bitcast: {
4654:     Register OpReg = I.getOperand(2).getReg();
4655:     SPIRVTypeInst OpType =
4656:         OpReg.isValid() ? GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(OpReg) : nullptr;
4657:     if (!GR.isBitcastCompatible(ResType, OpType))
4658:       report_fatal_error("incompatible result and operand types in a bitcast");
4659:     return selectOpWithSrcs(ResVReg, ResType, I, {OpReg}, SPIRV::OpBitcast);
4660:   }
4661:   case Intrinsic::spv_unref_global:
4662:   case Intrinsic::spv_init_global: {
4663:     MachineInstr *MI = MRI->getVRegDef(I.getOperand(1).getReg());
4664:     MachineInstr *Init = I.getNumExplicitOperands() > 2
4665:                              ? MRI->getVRegDef(I.getOperand(2).getReg())
4666:                              : nullptr;
4667:     assert(MI);
4668:     Register GVarVReg = MI->getOperand(0).getReg();
4669:     if (!selectGlobalValue(GVarVReg, *MI, Init))
4670:       return false;
4671:     // We violate SSA form by inserting OpVariable and still having a gMIR
4672:     // instruction %vreg = G_GLOBAL_VALUE @gvar. We need to fix this by erasing
4673:     // the duplicated definition.
4674:     if (MI->getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_GLOBAL_VALUE) {
4675:       GR.invalidateMachineInstr(MI);
4676:       MI->eraseFromParent();
4677:     }
4678:     return true;
4679:   }
4680:   case Intrinsic::spv_undef: {
4681:     auto MIB = BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpUndef))
4682:                    .addDef(ResVReg)
4683:                    .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType));
4684:     MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
4685:     return true;
4686:   }
4687:   case Intrinsic::spv_named_boolean_spec_constant: {
4688:     auto Opcode = I.getOperand(3).getImm() ? SPIRV::OpSpecConstantTrue
4689:                                            : SPIRV::OpSpecConstantFalse;
4690:
4691:     auto MIB = BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(Opcode))
4692:                    .addDef(I.getOperand(0).getReg())
4693:                    .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType));
4694:     MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
4695:     unsigned SpecId = I.getOperand(2).getImm();
4696:     buildOpDecorate(I.getOperand(0).getReg(), *++MIB->getIterator(), TII,
4697:                     SPIRV::Decoration::SpecId, {SpecId});
4698:
4699:     return true;
4700:   }
4701:   case Intrinsic::spv_const_composite: {
4702:     // If no values are attached, the composite is null constant.
4703:     bool IsNull = I.getNumExplicitDefs() + 1 == I.getNumExplicitOperands();
4704:     SmallVector<Register> CompositeArgs;
4705:     MRI->setRegClass(ResVReg, GR.getRegClass(ResType));
4706:
4707:     // skip type MD node we already used when generated assign.type for this
4708:     if (!IsNull) {
4709:       if (!wrapIntoSpecConstantOp(I, CompositeArgs))
4710:         return false;
4711:       std::function<bool(Register)> HasSpecConstOperand =
4712:           [&](Register Reg) -> bool {
4713:         MachineInstr *Def = MRI->getVRegDef(Reg);
4714:         if (!Def)
4715:           return false;
4716:         if (!isConstReg(MRI, Def))
4717:           return true;
4718:         // Recurse into not-yet-selected spv_const_composite intrinsics
4719:         // to detect transitive spec constant operands.
4720:         if (isSpvIntrinsic(*Def, Intrinsic::spv_const_composite)) {
4721:           for (unsigned J = Def->getNumExplicitDefs() + 1;
4722:                J < Def->getNumExplicitOperands(); ++J) {
4723:             if (Def->getOperand(J).isReg() &&
4724:                 HasSpecConstOperand(Def->getOperand(J).getReg()))
4725:               return true;
4726:           }
4727:         }
4728:         return false;
4729:       };
4730:       bool HasSpecConst = llvm::any_of(CompositeArgs, HasSpecConstOperand);
4731:       unsigned CompositeOpc = HasSpecConst ? SPIRV::OpSpecConstantComposite
4732:                                            : SPIRV::OpConstantComposite;
4733:       unsigned ContinuedOpc = HasSpecConst
4734:                                   ? SPIRV::OpSpecConstantCompositeContinuedINTEL
4735:                                   : SPIRV::OpConstantCompositeContinuedINTEL;
4736:       MachineIRBuilder MIR(I);
4737:       SmallVector<MachineInstr *, 4> Instructions = createContinuedInstructions(
4738:           MIR, CompositeOpc, 3, ContinuedOpc, CompositeArgs, ResVReg,
4739:           GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType));
4740:       for (auto *Instr : Instructions) {
4741:         Instr->setDebugLoc(I.getDebugLoc());
4742:         constrainSelectedInstRegOperands(*Instr, TII, TRI, RBI);
4743:       }
4744:       return true;
4745:     } else {
4746:       auto MIB = BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpConstantNull))
4747:                      .addDef(ResVReg)
4748:                      .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType));
4749:       MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
4750:       return true;
4751:     }
4752:   }
4753:   case Intrinsic::spv_assign_name: {
4754:     auto MIB = BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpName));
4755:     MIB.addUse(I.getOperand(I.getNumExplicitDefs() + 1).getReg());
4756:     for (unsigned i = I.getNumExplicitDefs() + 2;
4757:          i < I.getNumExplicitOperands(); ++i) {
4758:       MIB.addImm(I.getOperand(i).getImm());
4759:     }
4760:     MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
```

```cpp
5207: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectHandleFromBinding(Register &ResVReg,
5208:                                                        SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
5209:                                                        MachineInstr &I) const {
5210:   // The images need to be loaded in the same basic block as their use. We defer
5211:   // loading the image to the intrinsic that uses it.
5212:   if (ResType->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpTypeImage)
5213:     return true;
5214:
5215:   return loadHandleBeforePosition(ResVReg, GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(ResVReg),
5216:                                   *cast<GIntrinsic>(&I), I);
5217: }

5219: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectCounterHandleFromBinding(
5220:     Register &ResVReg, SPIRVTypeInst ResType, MachineInstr &I) const {
5221:   auto &Intr = cast<GIntrinsic>(I);
5222:   assert(Intr.getIntrinsicID() ==
5223:          Intrinsic::spv_resource_counterhandlefrombinding);
5224:
5225:   // Extract information from the intrinsic call.
5226:   Register MainHandleReg = Intr.getOperand(2).getReg();
5227:   auto *MainHandleDef = cast<GIntrinsic>(getVRegDef(*MRI, MainHandleReg));
5228:   assert(MainHandleDef->getIntrinsicID() ==
5229:          Intrinsic::spv_resource_handlefrombinding);
5230:
5231:   uint32_t Set = getIConstVal(Intr.getOperand(4).getReg(), MRI);
5232:   uint32_t Binding = getIConstVal(Intr.getOperand(3).getReg(), MRI);
5233:   uint32_t ArraySize = getIConstVal(MainHandleDef->getOperand(4).getReg(), MRI);
5234:   Register IndexReg = MainHandleDef->getOperand(5).getReg();
5235:   std::string CounterName =
5236:       getStringValueFromReg(MainHandleDef->getOperand(6).getReg(), *MRI) +
5237:       ".counter";
5238:
5239:   // Create the counter variable.
5240:   MachineIRBuilder MIRBuilder(I);
5241:   Register CounterVarReg =
5242:       buildPointerToResource(SPIRVTypeInst(GR.getPointeeType(ResType)),
5243:                              GR.getPointerStorageClass(ResType), Set, Binding,
5244:                              ArraySize, IndexReg, CounterName, MIRBuilder);
5245:
5246:   return BuildCOPY(ResVReg, CounterVarReg, I);
5247: }

5249: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectUpdateCounter(Register &ResVReg,
5250:                                                    SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
5251:                                                    MachineInstr &I) const {
5252:   auto &Intr = cast<GIntrinsic>(I);
5253:   assert(Intr.getIntrinsicID() == Intrinsic::spv_resource_updatecounter);
5254:
5255:   Register CounterHandleReg = Intr.getOperand(2).getReg();
5256:   Register IncrReg = Intr.getOperand(3).getReg();
5257:
5258:   // The counter handle is a pointer to the counter variable (which is a struct
5259:   // containing an i32). We need to get a pointer to that i32 member to do the
5260:   // atomic operation.
5261: #ifndef NDEBUG
5262:   SPIRVTypeInst CounterVarType = GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(CounterHandleReg);
5263:   SPIRVTypeInst CounterVarPointeeType = GR.getPointeeType(CounterVarType);
5264:   assert(CounterVarPointeeType &&
5265:          CounterVarPointeeType->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpTypeStruct &&
5266:          "Counter variable must be a struct");
5267:   assert(GR.getPointerStorageClass(CounterVarType) ==
5268:              SPIRV::StorageClass::StorageBuffer &&
5269:          "Counter variable must be in the storage buffer storage class");
5270:   assert(CounterVarPointeeType->getNumOperands() == 2 &&
5271:          "Counter variable must have exactly 1 member in the struct");
5272:   const SPIRVTypeInst MemberType =
5273:       GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(CounterVarPointeeType->getOperand(1).getReg());
5274:   assert(MemberType->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpTypeInt &&
5275:          "Counter variable struct must have a single i32 member");
5276: #endif
5277:
5278:   // The struct has a single i32 member.
5279:   MachineIRBuilder MIRBuilder(I);
5280:   const Type *LLVMIntType =
5281:       Type::getInt32Ty(I.getMF()->getFunction().getContext());
5282:
5283:   SPIRVTypeInst IntPtrType = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVPointerType(
5284:       LLVMIntType, MIRBuilder, SPIRV::StorageClass::StorageBuffer);
5285:
5286:   Register Zero = buildI32Constant(0, I);
5287:
5288:   Register PtrToCounter =
5289:       MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(IntPtrType));
5290:   BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpAccessChain))
5291:       .addDef(PtrToCounter)
5292:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(IntPtrType))
5293:       .addUse(CounterHandleReg)
5294:       .addUse(Zero)
5295:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
5296:
5297:   // For UAV/SSBO counters, the scope is Device. The counter variable is not
5298:   // used as a flag. So the memory semantics can be None.
5299:   Register Scope = buildI32Constant(SPIRV::Scope::Device, I);
5300:   Register Semantics = buildI32Constant(SPIRV::MemorySemantics::None, I);
5301:
5302:   int64_t IncrVal = getIConstValSext(IncrReg, MRI);
5303:   Register Incr = buildI32Constant(static_cast<uint32_t>(IncrVal), I);
5304:
5305:   Register AtomicRes = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(ResType));
5306:   BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpAtomicIAdd))
5307:       .addDef(AtomicRes)
5308:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
5309:       .addUse(PtrToCounter)
5310:       .addUse(Scope)
5311:       .addUse(Semantics)
5312:       .addUse(Incr)
5313:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
5314:   if (IncrVal >= 0) {
5315:     return BuildCOPY(ResVReg, AtomicRes, I);
5316:   }
5317:
5318:   // In HLSL, IncrementCounter returns the value *before* the increment, while
5319:   // DecrementCounter returns the value *after* the decrement. Both are lowered
5320:   // to the same atomic intrinsic which returns the value *before* the
```

```cpp
5862:   // https://registry.khronos.org/SPIR-V/specs/unified1/SPIRV.html#OpImageFetch
5863:   auto SampledOp = ImageType->getOperand(6);
5864:   bool IsFetch = (SampledOp.getImm() == 1);
5865:
5866:   auto AddOperands = [&](MachineInstrBuilder &MIB) {
5867:     uint32_t ImageOperandsMask = 0;
5868:     if (IsSignedInteger)
5869:       ImageOperandsMask |= 0x1000; // SignExtend
5870:
5871:     if (IsFetch && ImOps) {
5872:       if (ImOps->Lod)
5873:         ImageOperandsMask |= SPIRV::ImageOperand::Lod;
5874:       if (ImOps->Offset && !isScalarOrVectorIntConstantZero(*ImOps->Offset)) {
5875:         if (isConstReg(MRI, *ImOps->Offset))
5876:           ImageOperandsMask |= SPIRV::ImageOperand::ConstOffset;
5877:         else
5878:           ImageOperandsMask |= SPIRV::ImageOperand::Offset;
5879:       }
5880:     }
```

```cpp
5973: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectResourceNonUniformIndex(
5974:     Register &ResVReg, SPIRVTypeInst ResType, MachineInstr &I) const {
5975:   Register ObjReg = I.getOperand(2).getReg();
5976:   if (!BuildCOPY(ResVReg, ObjReg, I))
5977:     return false;
5978:
5979:   buildOpDecorate(ResVReg, I, TII, SPIRV::Decoration::NonUniformEXT, {});
5980:   // Check for the registers that use the index marked as non-uniform
5981:   // and recursively mark them as non-uniform.
5982:   // Per the spec, it's necessary that the final argument used for
5983:   // load/store/sample/atomic must be decorated, so we need to propagate the
5984:   // decoration through access chains and copies.
5985:   // https://docs.vulkan.org/samples/latest/samples/extensions/descriptor_indexing/README.html#_when_to_use_non_uniform_indexing_qualifier
5986:   decorateUsesAsNonUniform(ResVReg);
5987:   return true;
5988: }
5989:
5990: void SPIRVInstructionSelector::decorateUsesAsNonUniform(
5991:     Register &NonUniformReg) const {
5992:   llvm::SmallVector<Register> WorkList = {NonUniformReg};
5993:   while (WorkList.size() > 0) {
5994:     Register CurrentReg = WorkList.back();
5995:     WorkList.pop_back();
5996:
5997:     bool IsDecorated = false;
5998:     for (MachineInstr &Use : MRI->use_instructions(CurrentReg)) {
5999:       if (Use.getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpDecorate &&
6000:           Use.getOperand(1).getImm() == SPIRV::Decoration::NonUniformEXT) {
6001:         IsDecorated = true;
6002:         continue;
6003:       }
6004:       // Check if the instruction has the result register and add it to the
6005:       // worklist.
6006:       if (Use.getOperand(0).isReg() && Use.getOperand(0).isDef()) {
6007:         Register ResultReg = Use.getOperand(0).getReg();
6008:         if (ResultReg == CurrentReg)
6009:           continue;
6010:         WorkList.push_back(ResultReg);
6011:       }
6012:     }
6013:
6014:     if (!IsDecorated) {
6015:       buildOpDecorate(CurrentReg, *MRI->getVRegDef(CurrentReg), TII,
6016:                       SPIRV::Decoration::NonUniformEXT, {});
6017:     }
6018:   }
6019: }
```

```cpp
6021: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::extractSubvector(
6022:     Register &ResVReg, SPIRVTypeInst ResType, Register &ReadReg,
6023:     MachineInstr &InsertionPoint) const {
6024:   SPIRVTypeInst InputType = GR.getResultType(ReadReg);
6025:   [[maybe_unused]] uint64_t InputSize =
6026:       GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentCount(InputType);
6027:   uint64_t ResultSize = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentCount(ResType);
6028:   assert(InputSize > 1 && "The input must be a vector.");
6029:   assert(ResultSize > 1 && "The result must be a vector.");
6030:   assert(ResultSize < InputSize &&
6031:          "Cannot extract more element than there are in the input.");
6032:   SmallVector<Register> ComponentRegisters;
6033:   SPIRVTypeInst ScalarType = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentType(ResType);
6034:   const TargetRegisterClass *ScalarRegClass = GR.getRegClass(ScalarType);
6035:   for (uint64_t I = 0; I < ResultSize; I++) {
6036:     Register ComponentReg = MRI->createVirtualRegister(ScalarRegClass);
6037:     BuildMI(*InsertionPoint.getParent(), InsertionPoint,
6038:             InsertionPoint.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpCompositeExtract))
6039:         .addDef(ComponentReg)
6040:         .addUse(ScalarType->getOperand(0).getReg())
6041:         .addUse(ReadReg)
6042:         .addImm(I)
6043:         .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
6044:     ComponentRegisters.emplace_back(ComponentReg);
6045:   }
6046:
6047:   MachineInstrBuilder MIB = BuildMI(*InsertionPoint.getParent(), InsertionPoint,
6048:                                     InsertionPoint.getDebugLoc(),
6049:                                     TII.get(SPIRV::OpCompositeConstruct))
6050:                                 .addDef(ResVReg)
6051:                                 .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType));
6052:
6053:   for (Register ComponentReg : ComponentRegisters)
6054:     MIB.addUse(ComponentReg);
6055:   MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
6056:   return true;
6057: }
```

```cpp
6392: // Returns true iff `Ty` is a concrete SPIR-V type per the SPV_KHR_abort
6393: // definition: a numerical scalar (int/float), a (physical) pointer, a vector,
6394: // matrix or any aggregate (array/struct) recursively containing only such
6395: // types. OpTypeBool, OpTypeVoid, opaque handles and similar abstract
6396: // non-concrete types are rejected.
6397: static bool isConcreteSPIRVType(SPIRVTypeInst Ty,
6398:                                 const SPIRVGlobalRegistry &GR) {
6399:   SmallVector<SPIRVTypeInst, 4> Worklist{Ty};
6400:   while (!Worklist.empty()) {
6401:     SPIRVTypeInst T = Worklist.pop_back_val();
6402:     switch (T->getOpcode()) {
6403:     case SPIRV::OpTypeInt:
6404:     case SPIRV::OpTypeFloat:
6405:     case SPIRV::OpTypePointer:
6406:       break;
6407:     case SPIRV::OpTypeVector:
6408:     case SPIRV::OpTypeMatrix:
6409:     case SPIRV::OpTypeArray: {
6410:       Register OperandReg = T->getOperand(1).getReg();
6411:       SPIRVTypeInst ElementT = GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(OperandReg);
6412:       Worklist.push_back(ElementT);
6413:     } break;
6414:     case SPIRV::OpTypeStruct:
6415:       for (unsigned Idx = 1, E = T->getNumOperands(); Idx < E; ++Idx) {
6416:         Register OperandReg = T->getOperand(Idx).getReg();
6417:         SPIRVTypeInst ElementT = GR.getSPIRVTypeForVReg(OperandReg);
6418:         Worklist.push_back(ElementT);
6419:       }
6420:       break;
6421:     default:
6422:       return false;
6423:     }
6424:   }
6425:   return true;
6426: }
```

```cpp
6492: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectBranch(MachineInstr &I) const {
6493:   // InstructionSelector walks backwards through the instructions. We can use
6494:   // both a G_BR and a G_BRCOND to create an OpBranchConditional. We hit G_BR
6495:   // first, so can generate an OpBranchConditional here. If there is no
6496:   // G_BRCOND, we just use OpBranch for a regular unconditional branch.
6497:   const MachineInstr *PrevI = I.getPrevNode();
6498:   MachineBasicBlock &MBB = *I.getParent();
6499:   if (PrevI != nullptr && PrevI->getOpcode() == TargetOpcode::G_BRCOND) {
6500:     BuildMI(MBB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpBranchConditional))
6501:         .addUse(PrevI->getOperand(0).getReg())
6502:         .addMBB(PrevI->getOperand(1).getMBB())
6503:         .addMBB(I.getOperand(0).getMBB())
6504:         .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
6505:     return true;
6506:   }
6507:   BuildMI(MBB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpBranch))
6508:       .addMBB(I.getOperand(0).getMBB())
6509:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
6510:   return true;
6511: }
6512:
6513: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectBranchCond(MachineInstr &I) const {
6514:   // InstructionSelector walks backwards through the instructions. For an
6515:   // explicit conditional branch with no fallthrough, we use both a G_BR and a
6516:   // G_BRCOND to create an OpBranchConditional. We should hit G_BR first, and
6517:   // generate the OpBranchConditional in selectBranch above.
6518:   //
6519:   // If an OpBranchConditional has been generated, we simply return, as the work
6520:   // is alread done. If there is no OpBranchConditional, LLVM must be relying on
6521:   // implicit fallthrough to the next basic block, so we need to create an
6522:   // OpBranchConditional with an explicit "false" argument pointing to the next
6523:   // basic block that LLVM would fall through to.
6524:   const MachineInstr *NextI = I.getNextNode();
6525:   // Check if this has already been successfully selected.
6526:   if (NextI != nullptr && NextI->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpBranchConditional)
6527:     return true;
6528:   // Must be relying on implicit block fallthrough, so generate an
6529:   // OpBranchConditional with the "next" basic block as the "false" target.
6530:   MachineBasicBlock &MBB = *I.getParent();
6531:   unsigned NextMBBNum = MBB.getNextNode()->getNumber();
6532:   MachineBasicBlock *NextMBB = I.getMF()->getBlockNumbered(NextMBBNum);
6533:   BuildMI(MBB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpBranchConditional))
6534:       .addUse(I.getOperand(0).getReg())
6535:       .addMBB(I.getOperand(1).getMBB())
6536:       .addMBB(NextMBB)
6537:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
6538:   return true;
6539: }
6540:
6541: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectPhi(Register ResVReg,
6542:                                          MachineInstr &I) const {
6543:   auto MIB =
6544:       BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(TargetOpcode::PHI))
6545:           .addDef(ResVReg);
6546:   const unsigned NumOps = I.getNumOperands();
6547:   for (unsigned i = 1; i < NumOps; i += 2) {
6548:     MIB.addUse(I.getOperand(i + 0).getReg());
6549:     MIB.addMBB(I.getOperand(i + 1).getMBB());
6550:   }
6551:   MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
6552:   return true;
6553: }
```

```cpp
6671: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectLog10(Register ResVReg,
6672:                                            SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
6673:                                            MachineInstr &I) const {
6674:   if (STI.canUseExtInstSet(SPIRV::InstructionSet::OpenCL_std)) {
6675:     return selectExtInst(ResVReg, ResType, I, CL::log10);
6676:   }
6677:
6678:   // There is no log10 instruction in the GLSL Extended Instruction set, so it
6679:   // is implemented as:
6680:   // log10(x) = log2(x) * (1 / log2(10))
6681:   //          = log2(x) * 0.30103
6682:
6683:   MachineIRBuilder MIRBuilder(I);
6684:   MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
6685:
6686:   // Build log2(x).
6687:   Register VarReg = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(ResType));
6688:   BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpExtInst))
6689:       .addDef(VarReg)
6690:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
6691:       .addImm(static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::InstructionSet::GLSL_std_450))
6692:       .addImm(GL::Log2)
6693:       .add(I.getOperand(1))
6694:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
6695:
6696:   // Build 0.30103.
6697:   assert(ResType->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpTypeVector ||
6698:          ResType->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpTypeFloat);
6699:   // TODO: Add matrix implementation once supported by the HLSL frontend.
6700:   SPIRVTypeInst SpirvScalarType = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentType(ResType);
6701:   // The literal must match the precision of the scalar type, otherwise the
6702:   // OpConstant will contain non-zero high-order bits and fail SPIR-V
6703:   // validation when the type is narrower than 32 bits (e.g. half).
6704:   APFloat ScaleVal(0.30103);
6705:   bool LosesInfo;
6706:   ScaleVal.convert(
6707:       getZeroFP(GR.getTypeForSPIRVType(SpirvScalarType)).getSemantics(),
6708:       APFloat::rmNearestTiesToEven, &LosesInfo);
6709:   Register ScaleReg = GR.buildConstantFP(ScaleVal, MIRBuilder, SpirvScalarType);
6710:
6711:   // Multiply log2(x) by 0.30103 to get log10(x) result.
6712:   auto Opcode = ResType->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpTypeVector
6713:                     ? SPIRV::OpVectorTimesScalar
6714:                     : SPIRV::OpFMulS;
6715:   BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(Opcode))
6716:       .addDef(ResVReg)
6717:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
6718:       .addUse(VarReg)
6719:       .addUse(ScaleReg)
6720:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
```

```cpp
6730:
6731:   // On GLSL (Vulkan) targets, there is no integer-exponent power instruction.
6732:   // Lower as: Pow(base, OpConvertSToF(exp)).
6733:   if (STI.canUseExtInstSet(SPIRV::InstructionSet::GLSL_std_450)) {
6734:     Register BaseReg = I.getOperand(1).getReg();
6735:     Register ExpReg = I.getOperand(2).getReg();
6736:     Register FloatExpReg = MRI->createVirtualRegister(GR.getRegClass(ResType));
6737:     if (!selectOpWithSrcs(FloatExpReg, ResType, I, {ExpReg},
6738:                           SPIRV::OpConvertSToF))
6739:       return false;
6740:     return selectExtInst(ResVReg, ResType, I, GL::Pow,
6741:                          /*setMIFlags=*/true, /*useMISrc=*/false,
6742:                          {BaseReg, FloatExpReg});
6743:   }
6744:   return false;
6745: }
6746:
6747: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::selectModf(Register ResVReg,
6748:                                           SPIRVTypeInst ResType,
6749:                                           MachineInstr &I) const {
6750:   // llvm.modf has a single arg --the number to be decomposed-- and returns a
6751:   // struct { restype, restype }, while OpenCLLIB::modf has two args --the
6752:   // number to be decomposed and a pointer--, returns the fractional part and
6753:   // the integral part is stored in the pointer argument. Therefore, we can't
6754:   // use directly the OpenCLLIB::modf intrinsic. However, we can do some
6755:   // scaffolding to make it work. The idea is to create an alloca instruction
6756:   // to get a ptr, pass this ptr to OpenCL::modf, and then load the value
6757:   // from this ptr to place it in the struct. llvm.modf returns the fractional
6758:   // part as the first element of the result, and the integral part as the
6759:   // second element of the result.
6760:
6761:   // At this point, the return type is not a struct anymore, but rather two
6762:   // independent elements of SPIRVResType. We can get each independent element
6763:   // from I.getDefs() or I.getOperands().
6764:   if (STI.canUseExtInstSet(SPIRV::InstructionSet::OpenCL_std)) {
6765:     MachineIRBuilder MIRBuilder(I);
6766:     // Get pointer type for alloca variable.
6767:     const SPIRVTypeInst PtrType = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVPointerType(
6768:         ResType, MIRBuilder, SPIRV::StorageClass::Function);
6769:     // Create new register for the pointer type of alloca variable.
6770:     Register PtrTyReg =
6771:         MIRBuilder.getMRI()->createVirtualRegister(&SPIRV::iIDRegClass);
6772:     MIRBuilder.getMRI()->setType(
6773:         PtrTyReg,
6774:         LLT::pointer(storageClassToAddressSpace(SPIRV::StorageClass::Function),
6775:                      GR.getPointerSize()));
6776:
6777:     // Assign SPIR-V type of the pointer type of the alloca variable to the
6778:     // new register.
6779:     GR.assignSPIRVTypeToVReg(PtrType, PtrTyReg, MIRBuilder.getMF());
6780:     MachineBasicBlock::iterator VarPos = getOpVariableMBBIt(*I.getMF());
6781:     MachineBasicBlock &EntryBB = I.getMF()->front();
6782:     auto AllocaMIB =
6783:         BuildMI(EntryBB, VarPos, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpVariable))
6784:             .addDef(PtrTyReg)
6785:             .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(PtrType))
6786:             .addImm(static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::StorageClass::Function));
6787:     Register Variable = AllocaMIB->getOperand(0).getReg();
6788:
6789:     MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
6790:     // Create the OpenCLLIB::modf instruction.
6791:     auto MIB =
6792:         BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpExtInst))
6793:             .addDef(ResVReg)
6794:             .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
6795:             .addImm(static_cast<uint32_t>(SPIRV::InstructionSet::OpenCL_std))
6796:             .addImm(CL::modf)
6797:             .setMIFlags(I.getFlags())
6798:             .add(I.getOperand(I.getNumExplicitDefs())) // Floating point value.
6799:             .addUse(Variable); // Pointer to integral part.
6800:     // Assign the integral part stored in the ptr to the second element of the
6801:     // result.
6802:     Register IntegralPartReg = I.getOperand(1).getReg();
6803:     if (IntegralPartReg.isValid()) {
6804:       // Load the value from the pointer to integral part.
6805:       auto LoadMIB = BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpLoad))
6806:                          .addDef(IntegralPartReg)
6807:                          .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
6808:                          .addUse(Variable);
6809:       LoadMIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
6810:       return true;
6811:     }
6812:
6813:     MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
6814:     return true;
6815:   } else if (STI.canUseExtInstSet(SPIRV::InstructionSet::GLSL_std_450)) {
6816:     assert(false && "GLSL::Modf is deprecated.");
6817:     // FIXME: GL::Modf is deprecated, use Modfstruct instead.
6818:     return false;
6819:   }
6820:   return false;
6821: }
6822:
6823: // Generate the instructions to load 3-element vector builtin input
6824: // IDs/Indices.
6825: // Like: GlobalInvocationId, LocalInvocationId, etc....
6826:
6827: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::loadVec3BuiltinInputID(
6828:     SPIRV::BuiltIn::BuiltIn BuiltInValue, Register ResVReg,
6829:     SPIRVTypeInst ResType, MachineInstr &I) const {
6830:   MachineIRBuilder MIRBuilder(I);
6831:   const SPIRVTypeInst Vec3Ty =
6832:       GR.getOrCreateSPIRVVectorType(ResType, 3, MIRBuilder, false);
6833:   const SPIRVTypeInst PtrType = GR.getOrCreateSPIRVPointerType(
6834:       Vec3Ty, MIRBuilder, SPIRV::StorageClass::Input);
6835:
6836:   // Create new register for the input ID builtin variable.
6837:   Register NewRegister =
6838:       MIRBuilder.getMRI()->createVirtualRegister(&SPIRV::iIDRegClass);
6839:   MIRBuilder.getMRI()->setType(NewRegister, LLT::pointer(0, 64));
6840:   GR.assignSPIRVTypeToVReg(PtrType, NewRegister, MIRBuilder.getMF());
6841:
6842:   // Build global variable with the necessary decorations for the input ID
6843:   // builtin variable.
6844:   Register Variable = GR.buildGlobalVariable(
6845:       NewRegister, PtrType, getLinkStringForBuiltIn(BuiltInValue), nullptr,
6846:       SPIRV::StorageClass::Input, nullptr, true, std::nullopt, MIRBuilder,
6847:       false);
6848:
6849:   // Create new register for loading value.
6850:   MachineRegisterInfo *MRI = MIRBuilder.getMRI();
6851:   Register LoadedRegister = MRI->createVirtualRegister(&SPIRV::iIDRegClass);
6852:   MIRBuilder.getMRI()->setType(LoadedRegister, LLT::pointer(0, 64));
6853:   GR.assignSPIRVTypeToVReg(Vec3Ty, LoadedRegister, MIRBuilder.getMF());
6854:
6855:   // Load v3uint value from the global variable.
6856:   BuildMI(*I.getParent(), I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpLoad))
6857:       .addDef(LoadedRegister)
6858:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(Vec3Ty))
6859:       .addUse(Variable);
6860:
```
- EN: This range implements operational logic in helpers such as selectExtInst, getOperand, createVirtualRegister, MIRBuilder, translating backend policy into executable code.
- CN: 这一段实现了 selectExtInst、getOperand、createVirtualRegister、MIRBuilder 等操作逻辑，把后端策略落实为可执行的代码路径。

### Lines 6861-6984
```cpp
6861:   // Get the input ID index. Expecting operand is a constant immediate value,
6862:   // wrapped in a type assignment.
6863:   assert(I.getOperand(2).isReg());
6864:   const uint32_t ThreadId = foldImm(I.getOperand(2), MRI);
6865:
6866:   // Extract the input ID from the loaded vector value.
6867:   MachineBasicBlock &BB = *I.getParent();
6868:   auto MIB = BuildMI(BB, I, I.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(SPIRV::OpCompositeExtract))
6869:                  .addDef(ResVReg)
6870:                  .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
6871:                  .addUse(LoadedRegister)
6872:                  .addImm(ThreadId);
6873:   MIB.constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
6874:   return true;
6875: }

6884:       ResType, MIRBuilder, SPIRV::StorageClass::Input);

6889:   MIRBuilder.getMRI()->setType(
6890:       NewRegister,
6891:       LLT::pointer(storageClassToAddressSpace(SPIRV::StorageClass::Input),
6892:                    GR.getPointerSize()));

6894:

6899:       SPIRV::StorageClass::Input, nullptr, true, std::nullopt, MIRBuilder,

6904:                  .addDef(ResVReg)

6912: SPIRVTypeInst SPIRVInstructionSelector::widenTypeToVec4(SPIRVTypeInst Type,
6913:                                                         MachineInstr &I) const {
6914:   MachineIRBuilder MIRBuilder(I);
6915:   if (Type->getOpcode() != SPIRV::OpTypeVector)
6916:     return GR.getOrCreateSPIRVVectorType(Type, 4, MIRBuilder, false);
6917:
6918:   if (GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentCount(Type) == 4)
6919:     return Type;
6920:
6921:   SPIRVTypeInst ScalarType = GR.getScalarOrVectorComponentType(Type);
6922:   return GR.getOrCreateSPIRVVectorType(ScalarType, 4, MIRBuilder, false);
6923: }
6924:
6925: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::loadHandleBeforePosition(
6926:     Register &HandleReg, SPIRVTypeInst ResType, GIntrinsic &HandleDef,
6927:     MachineInstr &Pos) const {
6928:
6929:   assert(HandleDef.getIntrinsicID() ==
6930:          Intrinsic::spv_resource_handlefrombinding);
6931:   uint32_t Set = foldImm(HandleDef.getOperand(2), MRI);
6932:   uint32_t Binding = foldImm(HandleDef.getOperand(3), MRI);
6933:   uint32_t ArraySize = foldImm(HandleDef.getOperand(4), MRI);
6934:   Register IndexReg = HandleDef.getOperand(5).getReg();
6935:   std::string Name =
6936:       getStringValueFromReg(HandleDef.getOperand(6).getReg(), *MRI);
6937:
6938:   bool IsStructuredBuffer = ResType->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpTypePointer;
6939:   MachineIRBuilder MIRBuilder(HandleDef);
6940:   SPIRVTypeInst VarType = ResType;
6941:   SPIRV::StorageClass::StorageClass SC = SPIRV::StorageClass::UniformConstant;
6942:
6943:   if (IsStructuredBuffer) {
6944:     VarType = GR.getPointeeType(ResType);
6945:     SC = GR.getPointerStorageClass(ResType);
6946:   }
6947:
6948:   if (ResType->getOpcode() == SPIRV::OpTypeImage && ArraySize == 0)
6949:     MIRBuilder.buildInstr(SPIRV::OpCapability)
6950:         .addImm(SPIRV::Capability::RuntimeDescriptorArrayEXT);
6951:
6952:   Register VarReg =
6953:       buildPointerToResource(SPIRVTypeInst(VarType), SC, Set, Binding,
6954:                              ArraySize, IndexReg, Name, MIRBuilder);
6955:
6956:   // The handle for the buffer is the pointer to the resource. For an image, the
6957:   // handle is the image object. So images get an extra load.
6958:   uint32_t LoadOpcode =
6959:       IsStructuredBuffer ? SPIRV::OpCopyObject : SPIRV::OpLoad;
6960:   GR.assignSPIRVTypeToVReg(ResType, HandleReg, *Pos.getMF());
6961:   BuildMI(*Pos.getParent(), Pos, HandleDef.getDebugLoc(), TII.get(LoadOpcode))
6962:       .addDef(HandleReg)
6963:       .addUse(GR.getSPIRVTypeID(ResType))
6964:       .addUse(VarReg)
6965:       .constrainAllUses(TII, TRI, RBI);
6966:   return true;
6967: }

6969: bool SPIRVInstructionSelector::errorIfInstrOutsideShader(
6970:     MachineInstr &I) const {
6971:   if (!STI.isShader())
6972:     return diagnoseUnsupported(
6973:         I, "this instruction is only supported in shaders.");
6974:   return true;
6975: }
```

## Key Concepts / 关键概念

- EN: Instruction selectors bridge generic machine IR and backend-specific opcode choices.
  - CN: 指令选择器负责连接通用机器 IR 与后端特定的操作码选择。
- EN: SPIR-V backend code must preserve rich type information and encode capabilities/extensions explicitly.
  - CN: SPIR-V 后端需要保留丰富的类型信息，并显式编码 capability 与 extension。
- EN: Key symbols in this file include ImageOperands, SplitParts, getSelectionOperandForImm, llvm_unreachable, SPIRVInstructionSelector, which anchor the file's main abstractions.
  - CN: 该文件中的关键符号包括 ImageOperands, SplitParts, getSelectionOperandForImm, llvm_unreachable, SPIRVInstructionSelector，它们构成了本文件的核心抽象。
